## v6.36 — the sidearm decoder (보조무기 해독기)
- A third decoder bingo card, kind `side` (JS) / `side_*` (SQL): numbers 1..side_hi (80), 12 line items from `gacha_config.side_lines` = gun:bholemini, gun:crimson, coins 500/1000/1000/2000/3000, frags 20/20/30, tickets 1/1 (근하신년 decoders). Coins only: side_cost1 500 / side_cost10 4500, side_frag_min..max 1–2 per decoder, an owned gun's line pays side_owned_coins 5000. Shuffles: shuffle_free a day on its own counter (profiles.side_shuffle_day / side_shuffles).
- Server: table side_boards (RLS read own), qz_side_new / qz_side_json (internal), qz_side, qz_side_decode(p_count), qz_side_reset, qz_side_shuffle; gacha_log src 'side', coin_log 'side_decoder' / 'side_win'. bholemini and crimson are `sold=false, price 0` (not bought, not gifted, not exchanged); accounts that bought them keep them.
- Client: ACC.side + loadSide / sideDecode / sideReset / sideShuffle (ACC.sub is the subscribe method — do not reuse the name). shop.js: SHOP.bk('side') {s:1, lo:1}, shopState 'side', sideGun(), cardGuns(k), sideSvg() (a revolver cylinder `.cyl` that indexes a chamber at a time, spins while rolling), the `sd` crimson theme (style.css), sPopRules(kind) for both item cards, bingoSim(N).two = the later of two lines. test/mock.js mirrors qz_side* (__MOCK_DRW = the next sidearm card's items).
- While a batch plays out, the line tiles show as owned only the guns owned before it plus the ones revealed so far (SHOP.ownView, anim.own0) — gotDraws adds the won guns to ACC.me.owned before the animation.

## v6.38 — host migration (net.js; PeerJS rooms only)
- Line of succession: netMigOrder(NET.lob.pl, hostKey) = pl[1..] in join order, non-touch (p.t 0) before touch (p.t from hello `tc`; the host's own me.t in netLobbyCast). Every lobby cast also carries lob.pub (listed room) and lob.g (NET.gen, hand-over count). NET_VER bumped to -r7 so older pages cannot share a room.
- Triggers on a client: CLIH.bye without `why` (the host's NET.leave sends {t:'bye', mg:[order], g}), the host link closing / timing out (netLinkDown), or a host silent for 6 s in a match (frameEnd watchdog, not in the first 15 s after netBegin: NET.t0). netMigStart builds NET.mig {old (pl[0].k: the roster key; the link may be 'qzg-code' for bell joiners), list, gone, hold (in a match), role 'cli'}, drops the old link (a merely quiet one gets {t:'bye', why:'mig'} first), nulls the old host actor's a.net, clears NET.off / a.nb / lastK.
- netMigNext: first non-gone key in the list; mine → netMigPromote, else netMigDial(k) (PJ.peer.connect to that player's own peer id; every page's peer now takes connections: pjAccept). PeerJS 'peer-unavailable' (EXPIRE, ~5 s) → netMigGone → next. A dial not up in 14 s, or the link to the new host dropping → netFatal(mpMigFail). The hello carries mg / sk (gone keys) / wu (scenario upgrades).
- A client getting a hello with `mg` on a non-host link (netHandle routes it to netMigAsk): own host heard within 1.5 s (2.5 s lobby) → {t:'mignot'} (asker netFatal); else it starts its own migration, adds the asker's sk to gone and either promotes (first in line) or answers {t:'migto', k} (asker re-dials, ≤3 redirects; k = asker → it promotes).
- netMigPromote: NET.host, pl = [me, ...the rest not gone], M.want = their keys; ROOMS.pubOn = lob.pub; PJ.bellOn() after 1.5 s (a second Peer on 'qzg-<code>', retried every 5 s while taken, 30 tries: a crashed host's ghost holds it up to ~60 s; ROOMS retries a room_taken code every 20 s). NET.start (and NET.give) wait while NET.mig is set. In a match netMigTakeover: bots and the old host's actor get a.pup=false + AI.mk, remote players stay puppets, all L.inGame=false until their hello (netMigHello: L.inGame, got, a.wup, replies {t:'mig'}).
- netMigTick (host): all wanted players back (or gone from pl) or 15 s → netMigResume: no-shows → netDropPlayer (bots), 'mbot' (a.net=null for the old host / gone actors), netMigRestart, netEv('mgo', {w, n}), a chat line (CHAT.sys). Restart: infection prep/fight → G.round-- + startRound(); 'end' / 'over' carry on (over: netMigResults swaps in the to-lobby button); free scenario → SCEN.stageStart('retry') (lives kept); chapters → move: stageStart(st+1,'next'), boss stage: 'phase', board / ride / escape: carry on (SCEN.zsp recomputed), else 'retry' (terms/act keep progress). SCEN.best raised to the stages already cleared.
- The pause: netHold() (NET.mig.hold in a match) → Main.step skips playerCmd + gameUpdate (visuals only) and the host skips netHostTick; clients unpause on 'mgo'. Notice #migBox (centred in a match, top in the lobby; .done for 3.5 s after).
- 방장 넘기기 (lobby, host, PeerJS): slot button data-act="mpgive" (two taps) → NET.give(k): bye with mg [k, ...], PJ.detach (the code's id frees), becomes a client with NET.mig {why:'give', list:[k]}, a fresh PJ.mk() and dials k (it rejoins as a new player).
- PJ refactor: mk(id) (open → NET.me; 'connection' → pjAccept; reconnect on 'disconnected'), host(), join() (mk + dial, onErr routes errors during the join), bellOn/bellOff, detach() (returns the destroyer; NET.leave destroys after 150 ms so the goodbyes go out).

## v6.37 — invites (invite.js, after hof in the build)
- Room invites: table room_invites (from_id, to_id) → code/map/mode/at, 3 minutes to answer; qz_invite(p_who, p_code, p_map, p_mode) (friends only: inv_friends; same friend again after 10 s: inv_wait; 15 a minute: inv_many), qz_invites() (mine, newest first), qz_invite_answer(p_fc, p_code) (deletes). qz_ping and qz_friends carry `inv` (the count).
- Client INVT: polls qz_invites every 6 s only while signed in, in the lobby (G.st menu), not in a room, tab visible and with a friend online (frOn), plus at once when ping / friends report more invites than last seen. Pop-up #invPop (join → UI.act('mp') then 'mproom', no → answer). The waiting room (UI.mpLobby wrapper): 「친구 초대」 (FR.open with an invite button per friend) and 「초대 링크」 (?join=CODE&ref=FCODE; share sheet on touch). Empty seats invite too.
- Invite links: profiles.ref_by / ref_done, table referrals (referee pk, referrer, paid_at, rewarded, seen), gacha_config ref_new_coins 2000 / ref_new_tickets 2 / ref_coins 3000 / ref_tickets 2 / ref_matches 3 / ref_cap 10 / ref_days 7. qz_ref_set(p_code) (new accounts only: ref_old; once: ref_already; no self / loop; makes them friends), qz_ref_check() (after ACC.claim: pays both once matches ≥ ref_matches; locks the referrer row to keep the cap), qz_ref_info() (record; marks rewards seen). qz_me returns `ref` {by, done, left, n, paid, new}.
- Client REFL: ?ref= kept in localStorage qz_ref (7 days) → applied on the first signed-in emit; welcome / paid / inviter pop-ups (#refPop); the sign-up form shows the pending code; the friends window shows my link card (+ the room card while in a PeerJS room). The URL params are stripped at load.
- inv.js already owns the global INV (the inventory): the invite objects are INVT / REFL.

## v6.38.1
- Plasma Gun: plBoom multiplies by 2 when G.mode==='scen'. Thunderbolt marks skip targets without losClear. Balrog-XI: spShot resets spN when the gap since the last shell exceeds 1.5 shot intervals. EP.botPost: boss-stage stall breaker (B.stT/stN; botGoal gets i+stN).

## v6.39 — the firefighter zombie (fireman.js, last in the build)
- ZCLASS.fireman (skill 'charge', cd 15), appended to ZLIST (the 6th playable class) and to ZALL (network index 23). The model is design.js defZombie 'fireman'; the oxygen tank is NOT part of the rig: FM.place puts a GUNS.fmTank mesh (a.fmM, a child of ch.grp, matrixAutoUpdate off) on the back plate (ch.uB[1] × the rest offset) or, while charging, across the chest at a fixed body offset (FM_TY 1.22 up, FM_TZ .56 forward, axis = the body's right) so the model and the hit volume agree on every page (the local player's own rig is never computed).
- Coat (damageActor wrapper, host/offline only): fmFire (o.fire, o.dot = the burn tick from nyw.js updBurn, salamander/bdc/rdc/gas/ladle, Balrog-XI fireballs = w balrog11 with nocap) and fmBlast (o.he, gl40) → ×0.5.
- Charge: FM.start (host/offline, via nySkill; useSkill's list now includes 'charge') → FM.begin on every page (CLIH fmc {i,y}; the owner page uses its own yaw). The page that moves the body (host for bots, the player's own page) drives it in an actorPhysics wrapper: kv = heading × FM.SPD (12.5) with mv 0 and cmd zeroed (the Aleph charge pattern), heading turns ≤ FM.TURN (2.2 rad/s) toward a.yaw (bots: toward their target, and a.yaw = heading). A wall (moved < 35 % of the intended step for > .06 s after .15 s) ends it with a .45 s stagger; frost ends it; FM.DUR 1.4 s otherwise. Early ends go owner → host {t:'fmx'} → everyone (fmx {i,w}).
- Rams (host): every charging fireman (puppets while they move > 6.5 m/s on the host's view, plus a .4 s grace after the host's timer: fmGr) checks humans within hw+hw+.55 in front and at its height: SCEN.pushHuman (heading × 13 + 3.2 sideways, up 4.6; remote humans get 'scp') and FM.knock (fmKd 1.1 s; CLIH fmkd {i,t,s,d}). Each human once per charge (fmHit).
- Knocked flat (a.fmKd, every page): game.js updateVisual draws it with the get-up pose (an.dead = a.kdP, reviving flag); the actorPhysics / actorWeapons wrappers hold the body still and stop the gun; Main.camera wrapper drops the eye 1.1 m, rolls it and hides the view model. deadDir 1 = thrown backwards (on its back), -1 = hit from behind.
- The tank while charging: a.xRay (5 spheres r .13 along the axis) is tested by weapons.js rayActors and nyw.js rayActorsX after the usual volumes; within 15 cm of this body's own non-head hit the tank wins (the hands sit in front of it). shotTrace passes tank:0/1; projectiles fall back to the impact point next to the tank (FM.near). A client claims with bit 32 (net.js netClaimHit, judged on its own view; HOSTH.h decodes tank) and shows only a tank flash. Host: FM.tankHurt (380 hp; blasts hurt the tank too while the body takes its half) → FM.breakTank: charge over, stagger 1 s, skillCD and a.fmTkT = FM.RE (18 s, no tank drawn), ESMK cloud r 4.6 for 7 s (its own epsm event), shooter +1 score, CLIH fmb {i,s}.
- Smoke outside the chapters: FM.update runs ESMK.update when !EP.on; NY.clear (round start) clears it. ai.js: human bots skip zombies hidden by a cloud (epHid) for every zombie when !EP.on and a cloud exists (chapters keep the epHide-only rule).
- Bots: ai.js zombie skills — charge on the rush with a clear, walkable straight line (B.straight) 3.5–12.5 m from the target and a tank on its back.
- First person (vm.js claws + gunart.js buildClaws): the fireman's arms are 'sl_fmcoat' sleeves with an 'fmRefl' cuff over 'zs_fireman' hands; m.fmTank (scale .6) shows while FM.vmK(a) (= a.fmP) > 0 and the hands blend to FM.VMH. Atlas: zs_fireman + sl_fmcoat in gunart's list, fmRefl painted by fireman.js (119 of 120 slots used).
- NET_VER '-r8'.

## v6.39.1 — spectating clicks
- G.deathCam counts down past 0 (camera: `if(G.deathCam>0)G.deathCam-=dt`), so the old `!G.deathCam` test on the left click (main.js) and the touch fire button (touch.js) never passed once the death cam ended. Main.specOn() = dead, not in the menu, deathCam <= 0 and the camera is actually spectating (permaDead, or a human down); nextSpec (and the scenario's SCEN.H override) steps from the one watched now (L.indexOf(G.spec)+1), not from a stored index, so a changed list cannot land on the same player again.

## v6.40 — JANUS-1 (janus.js) and the Crowbar Maverick (crowbar.js), last in the build
- JANUS-1 (pistol slot, id janus1): WPN.noRel + supOnly — mag 5, res 0, no reload. loadout's buyCheck/buy are wrapped so the menu's ammo hides supOnly guns and buying the one in hand is 'own'; fillAmmo is wrapped with a.jnLeft (the rounds it held last time it was in the hands, reset by NY.clear at a round start), so dropping it and taking another does not refill it. Supply crates / chapter ammo boxes refill as before.
- The grenades are projectiles ('jn' / 'jnb', nyProj hook) that burst through detonate 'gl' with their own numbers (weapons.js: n.R, n.dmg, n.kb, n.stag, n.up, n.jw, n.he, n.vol/n.rate/n.shk; n.hitN = zombies caught); the coffin, KHEPRI and TIAMAT hooks scale by n.dmg. JN numbers: 130 a normal grenade, 150 in the form, ×2.5 in G.mode 'scen', radius 4.8.
- State (per actor): jnH counts (owner page, normal blasts with hitN>0, one per blast), jnS signal 35 s, jnO opening 0.62 s, jnB form 5 s, jnC closing 0.55 s (visual). Right click = W.alt 'janus' → nyAlt hook. In the form fireGun swaps in JN.WB (proj 'jnb', noMag) for the player and for replays; actorWeapons is wrapped: the drum reads >=1 during the call, pc.fire is cleared so a held trigger refires, nextFire = 60/214. Putting it away ends the form; death / turning / NY.clear reset everything. Network: ['jn', id, 0|1|2] fx (netDoFx hook) → jnRemote; remote pages run the timers themselves.
- Bots: AI.rollLd 7 % janus1 as sidearm; with the signal and a zombie in B.seen within 28 m they draw it and open it; an equip wrapper keeps it in hand while opening / open (or signalled with a zombie in sight); AI.update wrapper fires it while open.
- Models: GUNS.janus1 is the first-person model (tags cyl/cg drum and its glow, bU/bD barrel shells, fL/fR fins, crest, eye, aux hammer, gl inlays, h1..h5 charge pips; piv JN_PIV), GUNS.janus1n / s / b the world models (ensureGun hook picks by state; gunGeo follows G.wid so drops use janus1n). One new atlas slot: jnGlow (the atlas is now full: 120 of 120).
- vmGun (gunart.js) builds a group for every tag now — the Balrog-XI pips, Thunderbolt coils and switch, Balrog-5 gauge and Winchester lever had been dropped from the first-person model since they were made.
- Crowbar Maverick (melee, id cbmav): dmg [80,210], rate [.55,1.15], range [2.55,2.45], cleave [2,1], blunt. The heavy (o.heavy with w cbmav) shocks: damageActor hook on the host / solo → cbShock (3 s, bosses 0.6 s) + netEv 'cbsh' → cbShockOn on every page. a.shockT: maxSpeed ×0.12, no jump (actorPhysics hook), no skill (actorWeapons hook), a firefighter's charge ends; sparks / crackle / blue tint (updateVisual hook) on every page; the shocked player gets #hud.shk. The striking page shows cbZapFx at once.
- Its first-person swings are knife-engine clips (vmknife knGrip/KN_ELB): forehand (side -1, first) / backhand (side 1) as offsets; the stab keys are camera-space poses turned into offsets (cbKey). One fist (kaHand); W.drKey 'drCb' picks its own draw keys (vm.js). Bots: AI.rollLd 6 % cbmav.

## v6.41 — THANATOS-9 (thanatos.js), the M4A1 Dark Knight (m4dk.js) and transcendence fragments, last in the build
- Atlas: GA.H 1920 → 2048 (16 rows, 128 slots; it was full at 120). New materials: thSteel (THANATOS blade steel), dkPlate (black lacquer with sparse gold scrollwork — no border in the tile, since faces sample it at random places), dkGlow (self-lit crimson).
- weapons.js: rayActors reports a zombie's arm as part 'arm' (every other consumer treats it as the body); shotTrace multiplies legs / arms by W.limbK when a gun has one (the Dark Knight: 50/90).
- THANATOS-9 (melee, id thanatos9; numbers in THN — TH is the team constant): the generic melee code never sees its buttons (actorWeapons wrapper clears cmd.fire/alt around the call, thInput runs them on the owner page). Left click in the scythe form = meleeSwing (light, 'm' fx) + meleePend → meleeStrike hook → thSweepHit: meleeHits with cone -1.01 (360°, LOS from the eye), pushed radially. State a.thS 0 scythe / 1 transforming (THN.ON 3 s) / 2 drill (kept across weapon swaps) / 3 barrage (THN.BAR 5 s, a strike every THN.BI at up to THN.BN in a cone of THN.BCOS within THN.BR; equip and playerDrop blocked; becomes 4 by itself) / 4 folding back (THN.OFF). Swapping away during 1 or 4 drops it to 0. Network: ['th', id, st] from the owner; other pages run timers and show the strikes under NET.ghost and wait for the owner's next state.
- Weak stagger: a damageActor hook (host / solo, only when the hit took something off) puts staggerT back and sets t.thSlow (×THN.SLOWK in maxSpeed); EFF_T got ['thSlow','ts'] and netApplyEff applies e.ts, so a zombie another page runs gets it. Deadshot doubles both attacks (hs + ds: the host's window check divides by W.hs = 2 when it fails).
- First person: the model's six plates (tags b0..b5, each the same bowed plate thPlate, placed along a circle through the hub top TH.C) turn about their crescent roots; VMX.thanatos9 poses everything from one timeline T (thT: 0 scythe .. 1 drill): the pivot (TH.TK camera-space keys → rest-relative quaternions, fdTrack), both fists (kaHand toward the keyed elbows), the plates (thPlatePose: off the crescent tip first into a fan round the hub, a full turn, folded forward into the drill; in the barrage the drill spins and the two sets of three thrust by turns), the core. Sweeps are katana-style pivot clips (thClip, TH_SW / TH_FX), the trail violet. World models th9w / th9wb swapped by ensureGun (T > .8); third person: Skull-9's sweep, KF.thB a looping thrust for the barrage (atkD .5, restarted every strike).
- THANATOS bots (AI.update wrapper): sweep in reach, ready the drill when nothing is within 12 m, start the barrage at THN.BR − .3 with a clear line and walk into the target through it. Sounds: B.am needs a gain node of its own — an envelope's gain already has ramps, setValueCurveAtTime over them throws and the whole sound is left unrendered (only a console warning at boot).
- M4A1 Dark Knight (rifle, id m4dk): 50 / 300, dmg 90, hs 150/90, limbK 50/90, 700 rpm (the engine's per-frame firing makes 600 at 60 fps). Right click = W.alt 'dk' → dkSet (['dk', id, 0|1] fx). In B fireGun swaps in DK.WB (rail + dkp): tbShot is wrapped so dkShot traces the line instead — up to DK.WALLS walls (×DK.WALLK), every zombie on it (dkLine: head, body, legs, arm, boss volumes, the firefighter's tank; ×DK.PK per one before), a crimson beam on TB.beams; the actorWeapons wrapper sets nextFire to DK.BRPM. Half zoom: Main.camera wrapper (FOV ×DK.FOV, like hskill.js). Equip away / turning / NY.clear end B. Remote 'f' replays use B while that player's dkB is on.
- Deadshot +DK.DS s for an owner (dkOwns: the account; accounts off: in hand; bots: carrying it; others: a.dkOwn). A client's {t:'hsk',k:'ds'} carries x when it owns one (netToHost wrapper), HOSTH.hsk stores it, netEv('hsk') passes x on, CLIH.hsk stores it for the others; HSK.start adds the second.
- Transcendence (schema.sql v6.41): profiles.trans_frags; gacha_config trans_frag_min/max (2/4, every decoder of every card, 'tfrags' per draw and in the answers), trans_cost 888, trans_guns '{m4dk}'; qz_trans_exchange(p_gun) (not_for_exchange / already_owned / not_enough_tfrags; gacha_log src 'trans'). gun_prices: thanatos9 15,000 sold, m4dk 0 not sold (qz_buy / qz_gift refuse it). Client: ACC.me.tfrags, ACC.transExchange, shop page 'tr' (SHOP.trPage), shopState 'trans', lock tag 「초월」; the mock does the same.
- NET_VER -r10.

## v6.42 — the admin page (admin.html: admin/admin.js + admin.css), server notices and bans in the game (srvnote.js), play time
- The admin page is a page of its own (build.py writes dist/admin.html and dist/standalone/admin.html; copy the standalone one to the repo root next to index.html; Vercel's cleanUrls serves it at /admin, <meta robots noindex>). Built from sb.js (the Supabase project: SB moved out of account.js so both pages share it) + GUN_NAMES / MAP_NAMES read out of the game's sources by regex (WPN entries' n:[ko,en] and kind; the MAPDEFS literal) + admin/admin.js + admin/admin.css + Galmuri11 subset to the page's own characters (inlined). No game code.
- It has no powers of its own: every call is a qz_admin_* function, each starts with qz_admin_uid() (raises not_admin unless auth.uid() is in public.admins). Admins are set only by hand in the SQL Editor (the line is in schema.sql's admin section; the page's "not an admin" screen shows it with the signed-in e-mail filled in). Its session is its own (localStorage qz_adm_ses, password or SB.oauth sign-in) so the game and the page never refresh one token at the same time.
- Views (hash routes): #/dash (qz_admin_dash: the scoreboard — online now = last_seen within 150 s, the Korean clock, bans —, today's players / sign-ups / time, three 14-day column charts with hover, focus and a table view, today's coins and decoders, the public rooms), #/players[/<id>] (qz_admin_users search + sorts, 50 a page; the player sheet: qz_admin_user, grant / take back coins · fragments · transcendence fragments · decoders (qz_admin_grant, never below 0, coins also on coin_log 'admin'), guns (qz_admin_gun), nickname (qz_admin_rename), ban 1/3/7/30 days or for good (qz_admin_ban: rooms and invites go; admins cannot be) / unban, play time, record, coin / decoder / admin logs), #/settings/prices (qz_admin_price / _reset: admin_set rows keep their price and shop flag when schema.sql runs again, file_price / file_sold keep the file's; tier and transcendence guns cannot be sold), #/settings/gacha (qz_admin_config: any gacha_config column by name, the value cast by jsonb_populate_record, checked as a whole row afterwards — min ≤ max, card sizes, the lines' format and guns, trans_guns exist), #/settings/missions (qz_admin_mission_save: mission_pool.edited rows are left alone by later runs, the win3 migration included), #/notices (qz_admin_notice_save / _delete, a preview in the lobby strip's look), #/logs (qz_admin_logs, 100 a page). Every action on a player needs a reason; the destructive ones ask first (the effect spelled out).
- Bans: profiles.banned_until ('infinity' = for good) / ban_reason. qz_guard(uid) starts every call that pays, sells, opens decoders, posts a room or reaches another player (26 functions + qz_set_nickname); reading (qz_me, missions, the friend list, messages) and qz_ping still work; qz_ranking leaves banned accounts out. qz_me carries admin and ban {until, forever, reason}. In the game: srvnote.js opens a window once per sign-in and tags the profile; errText('banned').
- Server notices: public.notices (info / warn / event, popup, starts_at, ends_at, active); qz_notices() for anyone (the lobby: the first as a strip under the top bar — on a phone too, where the notice list is hidden —, all at the head of the notice list, a popup one in a window once per browser: localStorage qz_ntc; the ban window comes first).
- Play time: qz_ping(p_play integer default 0, p_fresh boolean default false) — the old qz_ping() is dropped. The time since the last beat from any tab counts (≤ 150 s; a longer gap is a new stretch) into profiles.online_secs, p_play (the game's own count of seconds in a match, a 1 s sampler while the tab is in view, capped by that time) into play_secs, and both into daily_activity (Korean day × player: the daily player counts). friends.js: a beat every minute while in view, a keepalive one as the tab leaves view (ACC.beacon), a fresh one on coming back or signing in. ACC.ping falls back to no arguments on an older database.
- Tests: a local Postgres 16 cluster with a Supabase-like prelude (anon / authenticated roles, auth.users, auth.uid() from test.uid) runs schema.sql (fresh, upgrade from v6.41, re-run) and SQL checks as role authenticated; a small Python bridge (PostgREST-style rpc with named, typed arguments + password tokens) lets the harness drive admin.html (PAGE=admin.html) and the game (QZ_SB) against that database. play.py takes PAGE and VIEW=WxH (not committed).

## v6.43 — admin page part two: mail, coupons, suspects, taking rooms down, staff (the game: mailbox.js)
- Two admin roles: public.admins.role 'owner' (set by hand in the SQL Editor, as before) or 'staff' (added on #/staff by an owner: qz_admin_staff_add by e-mail / friend code / unique nickname — not a banned account —, qz_admin_staff_remove; owners cannot be removed there). qz_admin_owner() (raises owner_only) starts grant, gun, prices, price / price_reset, config_get / config, missions / mission_save, notices / notice_save / notice_delete, every mail and coupon function and the staff ones; staff keep the dashboard, players (no e-mails: qz_admin_users / _user leave them out and the search skips them), rename, ban / unban (never an admin), suspects, room close and the log. The page hides the owner menus for staff (nav, routes, the sheet's grant / guns / mail link) and tags the role.
- Mail: public.mails (title ≤ 60, body ≤ 500, rewards, to_all — the accounts there when it was sent —, for_new — accounts made later too —, expires_at, active) + mail_targets (to chosen players) + mail_claims (once per account). qz_rewards_clean(rw) is the one check for a reward bundle (coins ≤ 10,000,000, frags ≤ 100,000, tfrags ≤ 10,000, tickets / season_tickets ≤ 1,000, ≤ 10 guns that exist and are not free; whole numbers ≥ 0; bad_rewards / no_gun), qz_give(uid, rw, why, det) pays it (coins also on coin_log 'mail' / 'coupon' — outside the daily cap and the suspects lists; owned guns are skipped and reported as had). qz_mail_open(uid) = the letters an account can take now; qz_mails() / qz_mail_claim(p_id default null = all) for players (qz_guard); qz_me mail_new, qz_ping mail (the count). #/mail: write a letter (all players / chosen ones, one per line, who_not_found names the misses), the sent list with taken / reached counts and an on / off switch (qz_admin_mail_active).
- Coupons: public.coupons (code ^[A-Z0-9]{4,20}$ — typed codes are upper-cased and stripped of anything else —, rewards, max_uses, uses, starts_at, expires_at, active, note) + coupon_uses + coupon_fails (10 wrong codes in 10 minutes → coupon_slow). qz_coupon_redeem answers its refusals as {error: coupon_*} instead of raising, so a wrong code's coupon_fails row is kept. An empty code on #/mail/coupons makes a random 10-character one; a coupon's rewards are fixed once someone used it; qz_admin_coupon_uses lists who.
- Suspects (#/suspects/<kind>, qz_admin_suspects): cap (days near the 20,000 daily cap in 14 days, from coin_log match / round), kills / damage (the highest single match / round in 14 days, with the average), rate (coins per hour of match time, ≥ 1 h played), refs (referrers with many thin invitees in 30 days). Only lists: nothing is decided from them.
- Taking a public room down: qz_admin_room_close(p_code, p_minutes 10..10080, p_reason) deletes the row and puts the host in public.room_blocks (until); qz_room_up raises room_closed for a blocked host. net.js: ROOMS.shutT — the room goes on privately and is not posted again for 5 minutes (a note once).
- The game (mailbox.js, built last): a letter button in the account chip after the coins with the count; #mailWin lists the letters (rewards as chips: COIN / FRAG / TFRAG / ticket svg / gun icons), 받기 / 모두 받기, the coupon box at the bottom. A new letter (id above localStorage qz_mailSeen) opens it once in the lobby when no other window is open — server notices, the ban window and the new-content window go first; a MutationObserver on body's children asks again when one of those windows closes; qz_mails is asked only when the count changed, at most every 20 s. Not while banned (the claim says banned). PGRST202 → MBOX.off (an older database: no button).
- test/mock.js: __MOCK_MAILS [{id,title,body,rewards,expires_at}] and __MOCK_COUPONS {CODE: rewards}; qz_me mail_new, qz_ping mail.
- Tests: SQL (staff refusals, no e-mails for staff, mail to all / chosen / for_new / expired / off / once, coupons normalize / used / sold out / slow / off / rewards fixed after use, room close + block, suspects, staff add / remove rules; fresh, upgrade from v6.41 and v6.42, re-run), the admin page as owner and as staff (bridge), the game's mailbox against the bridge (notices first, auto-open, take all, coupons and their errors, a new letter by heartbeat, a phone width) and against the mock (ban), plus the solo-mode and notice regressions and the boot check.

## v6.44 — the survivors wear masks (design.js defHuman + models.js painters)
- No bare faces: 박재원 (guard) a black knit balaclava (skin material = knit, so the neck and the back of the head are knit too) under a riot helmet with a lowered ballistic visor in three panels (centre + two wings at ry ±.6, hinges and side arms on the helmet); 엘레나 (medic) a teal-grey neck gaiter pulled up to the goggles (face kind 'gaiter': hair above the hem, the strap round the sides), goggles, a white half-mask respirator (rsp + rspN nose bridge, two round filters at ry ±.45, a valve with a green cross), the ponytail kept; 메이슨 (soldier) a coyote shemagh (decor op 'check' on the head, the neck and a rounded 'wrap' part round the lower head, a hanging tail) and the goggles pulled down over the eyes; 서유나 (hazmat) an M50-style mask in the suit's hood (face kind 'm50': one wide lens that dips over the nose, the hood's rim), cheek filters at ry ±.55 and a voicemitter. Helmets were made deep and tall enough that the head never shows through (the medic's helmet front had the hair poking through). References: GSG 9 / KNP SOU visors and balaclavas, desert shemagh + goggles, the US M50 mask; the shapes and paint are our own.
- models.js: face kinds 'balaclava' / 'gaiter' / 'm50' (+ FACE_GA, the gloss a painted face starts with), decor ops 'line', 'disc', 'check', 'solid', 'grad'; parts may carry fr (fillet radius as a share of their thinnest side, rounder filters) and the human extras now keep d / rx / ry / rz; paintSkin looks for the densest atlas scale in 3% steps instead of 10% (guard and soldier 292 px/m with the new parts instead of falling to 270; brute 200 → 218, coffin 241 → 250, fireman 243 → 257).
- Everything that draws a survivor follows: the 3D models (players, bots), portraits (lobby, inventory, room slots), the chapter 4 mirage and LYREBIRD's disguises. First-person arms, stats and hitboxes are unchanged; skins keep their keys, so mixed versions in one room are fine.
- Checked with a mugshot rig (the menu demo frozen, the four rest-pose models in front of the menu camera, front / 3-4 / side / back) and live bots in a match (single frames drawn with the loop stopped: a full match frame is too slow for screenshots here), portraits at 30–150 px, the solo modes and the boot check.

## v6.45 — the survivors go by their units
- HSKIN_N (game.js): KNP SOU (guard, Korean National Police SOU), PJ (medic, USAF Pararescue), LEGION (soldier, French Foreign Legion), CBIRF (hazmat, USMC CBIRF) — the same English names in both languages. HSKIN_R: a short unit line under the name on the inventory cards (like a zombie's skill line); HSKIN_T: the longer "ex-… · role" line as the hover title on every character card (inventory, room setup, multiplayer). Lore: RT-2 is a recovery team drawn from special units around the world.
- Skin keys (guard / medic / soldier / hazmat) are unchanged: saved picks, multiplayer and the models are not touched. design.js comments use the new names. No unit emblems or logos anywhere; CS's own unit names (GSG-9, SAS, GIGN, SEAL) were avoided, SEAL also because the US Navy holds it as a trademark.

## v6.46 — every weapon that comes in shows on screen (gunpop.js)
- One window for weapons that arrive outside the shop and the decoders: #gunPop (the gun's picture at 52 px, its name and kind, where it came from, OK; above the mailbox at z 9600; Escape / OK / the backdrop close it; a match start removes it). GUNPOP.show(guns, src) — src {mail: title} / {coupon: code} / null (an admin). Friends' gifts keep their own window.
- An admin's direct grant (qz_admin_gun give) skips the mailbox, so the server keeps public.gun_notices (id, user_id, gun_id, src 'admin', at, seen; RLS on, no direct access); taking the gun back before it was seen deletes the notice. qz_me gun_new and the heartbeat's gun count say how many wait; GUNPOP.check (on account changes, a lobby build, and when a window in the way closes — the same MutationObserver idea as the mailbox) lists them with qz_gun_notices (newest first, 20), shows them in one window, marks them with qz_gun_seen(ids) (seen ones older than 30 days are deleted) and reloads qz_me so the shop and the inventory have the gun at once. It waits for the lobby with nothing else open (MBOX.busyUI, which now counts #gunPop; the new-content window waits for #gunPop and #mailWin).
- A letter or a coupon with a gun opens the window right when it is taken (MBOX.claim / MBOX.redeem, on top of the mailbox).
- Admin page: the reward editor's gun picker puts a gun in the moment it is picked (first line 「무기 넣기 — 고르면 바로 들어가요」, then back to it; the old separate button was easy to miss, so a gun picked but not added was left out of the letter); the player sheet says a direct grant skips the mailbox and opens the window in the game; its toast says so too.
- Fixed: the gift and invite windows used the round-result animation, whose translate(-50%) pushed a flex-centred box half its width to the left (new popIn keyframes).
- Tests: SQL (a notice on a grant, none twice, taking back removes an unseen one, qz_me / qz_ping counts, listing, seen by ids and all, another account's ids do nothing, the table closed, anon refused; fresh, upgrade, re-run), the game against the bridge (a grant while the mailbox is open waits and shows two guns when it closes, a letter with a gun, a coupon with a gun, owned guns updated), the admin page (pick to add, duplicates refused), the mock (notices on sign-in), the mailbox regression and the boot check.

## v6.47 — THANATOS-9's barrage lasts 3 s
- THN.BAR 5 → 3 (thanatos.js): the drill form's barrage is 3 s of strikes, one every THN.BI (0.1 s) as before, so THN.NMAX = 30 strikes (was 50). Damage per strike, reach, cone, the lock (no cancel, no weapon change) and the fold-back are unchanged.
- Everything that said 5 s now reads THN.BAR: the drill-ready note, the weapon tags (buy menu / inventory), the barrage sound (thbar: its length, the drone's hold, the scream and one chatter grain per strike all follow THN.BAR and THN.NMAX). The new-content window's text says 3 s.

## v6.48 — 대인전: 팀 데스매치 (BLUE vs RED) · 개인전 (pvp.js, built last)
- Modes 'tdm' / 'ffa' (CFG.mode, the room's cfg.mode). Everyone stays team TH; who may hurt whom is foe(a,b) (game.js): G.pvp ? a.side!==b.side : a.team!==b.team — used by rayActors, meleeHit(s), airBlast, the radar, pings (pingTc: 's'+side) and team chat. Sides come from the roster on every page (PVP.assign: players — a.net, or the player alone — alternate 0/1 by id, bots fill the smaller side; 개인전: side = id + 2), so no message carries them.
- Flow: startMatch wrapper sets G.pvp, filters BUY_MENU (PVP.menu: only PVP.GUNS — the plain guns, knife / axe / hammer, HE, flare, armour, ammo; empty categories dropped, restored by PVP.off on toTitle / netToLobby / a non-PvP match) and DROPS.LIFE 30; alone the room window's CFG.pbots / pg (goal index) / ptime go into cfg. startRound → PVP.begin(plan): 5 s warm-up (G.st 'prep', nobody moves or shoots: Main.playerCmd wrapper, bots only look), placements (팀 데스매치: two bases — the human and zombie spawns when their centroids are ≥ 35 m apart, else the two open nav nodes farthest apart that a path joins; 개인전: farthest-point spread), sent as the usual 'round' plan. Then 'fight' for PVP.time (300 / 480 / 600 s); the first to the goal (팀 40 / 60 / 100, 개인 15 / 25 / 40; G.score = the teams' kills) or the most kills at the clock wins (팀: a tie is a draw, G.winner −1; 개인: leader by kills, then fewer deaths, then damage). 'end' 7 s with the card (#hRRes, the match's round reward on it: shopRoundReward, stats wrapped — won = my side / me, no infections), then 'over' and the results (patched: score, MVP, tables by side).
- Death and respawn (host): PVP.kill (ldSnap, no death drop, feed, kills / team score / assists ≥ 35 damage, killcam 1.4 s then the killer, streak calls) → a.pvpRT 3 s → PVP.spawnFor: the side's base (+22) unless an enemy is within 10 m, else the best of 16 open nodes and the map's spawns by distance to enemies (FFA ×1, TDM ×0.5), near teammates, not a spot used in the last 4 s, then the first of the top 7 no enemy within 50 m sees; facing the enemy base / the nearest enemy. PVP.respawn: fresh state, the noted loadout cleaned (sanitize), armour 100 always, 2 s protection (ends at the first shot; white shimmer), 12 s buy window (buyCheck 'pvpT' after it; B / sets while down say so). Bots re-roll their loadout 15 % of the time.
- Damage (PVP.hurt, host): fight only, foes only, no self damage, protection blocks; PVP.scale: headshots ×4/3 on top of the guns' ×3 (= ×4), melee ×0.85 / axe 0.8 / hammer 0.6; armour takes (1 − PVP.ap) of each hit while it lasts (sniper .97, rifle .78, mg .8, smg .62, pistol .65 (≥ 50 dmg .8), shotgun .72, melee .85, HE .6). HE in PvP: PVP.heBlast 98·(1 − d/6.5), line of sight, not the thrower or his side. Clients: netClaimHit shows the same numbers (PVP.scale / ap) and skips protected or friendly targets; the host checks each claim (PVP.claimOk: an allowed weapon, ≤ one hit's most (×1.05), a token bucket at the gun's rate × 1.6; HE ≤ 100 even after the thrower died). A hit on a remote player sends it 'phit' (the hit-direction arc).
- Network: host events pgo (fight), pk (kill: victim, killer, weapon, hs, dir, assist ids), prs (respawn place), pend (winner + score), the usual round / over; the snapshot's g[3], g[4] carry the team scores to clients, S stats carry assists. netOnState wrapper: right after a remote player's respawn the host ignores his reports from the old place (a.pvpSp) until he reports the new one (≤ 2 s). Host migration continues the match (netMigRestart → 'go'; bases are lost, respawns go dynamic).
- Bots (AI.pvp → PVP.bot): sight at ~8 Hz (70 m, ~140° FOV, line to body or head, the one that just hurt it even behind), shots heard within 40 m give a rough place; a target → hold the gun's range (shotgun 3–9, smg 5–16, rifle / mg 8–30, sniper 14–60, pistol 5–18), strafe, crouch at range, rare hops, HE at 7–18 m; aim like the zombie-mode humans with PvP-scaled difficulty (react ×1.7 + 0.08, err ×1.6, turn ×0.8, hsP ×0.55), short bursts past 18 m, snipers zoom; no target → last known / heard place, teammates' sightings (팀), the enemy base, shooting (개인), open ground. Loadouts rolled from the plain guns (rifles most).
- HUD (HUD.update wrapper): the top bar (mode · goal · my side / the two scores or my kills vs the best other, alive counts or my rank), countdown, damage, the death text (back in N s, killer's weapon and health left, spectating), aim info (names in side colours, teammates' health), #pvTag (spawn protection, buy window), teammate chevrons with names within 28 m (#pvMarks), hit-direction arcs (#pvHit), kill feed in side colours, Tab board by side with K / D / A. Bodies carry the side's colour (uTint, scaled by the light probe so it does not glow in the dark).
- Lobby: OPT.mode gains tdm / ffa (modeKey for names and rules); the room window shows bots 3–11 (CFG.pbots), goal and time instead of rounds / time / blackouts, no zombie class picker; the waiting room offers 0–12 bots, goal, time. Room list, invites and the admin page name the modes. Rewards need no schema change (qz_round_claim / qz_claim accept any mode but scen / range; rounds = minutes played, at most 10).
- Tests (single player only — the user tests multiplayer himself): bot-vs-bot TDM on Italy (spawn bases 61 m apart; 12 players reached 50 in 162 s, every bot moving and scoring), TDM on Q7 (dynamic bases 68 m apart), FFA on Q7 at normal and expert, no friendly fire, no hits on protected players, the end card, results, board, buy lock and window, the warm-up freeze, side tints on a day and a night map, leaving to the title and a Mutation match after it (buy menu restored), the room windows in every mode.

## v6.49 — 대인전 전용맵 「항만 터미널 Harbor Terminal」 (mapport.js)
- A container port on a clear afternoon, 86 × 72 m (bounds −46…44 × −38…38), made for PvP: MAPDEFS.port.pvp = 1. mapOk(mode, id) / mapsFor(mode) (map.js): a pvp map is offered only in the PvP modes — OPT.map(mode) in the room window and the waiting room, epMapOf falls back to the mode's first map, NET.start checks the room's map, a mode change moves to a map the new mode can use, picking a pvp map from the title list makes the mode 팀 데스매치. MAPLIST() is unchanged (title list, map count, NET_VER).
- Layout (x east to the quay, z south): defenders' start (MAP.spawns, yaw π) at the operations building on the north edge, attackers' start (MAP.zspawns) at the truck gate in the south; one road along the north edge joins the defenders to both sites. A long: from the upper yard (SE) through the long doors onto the quay and north under the ship's side. Mid: the customs lane (an X-ray portal with a truck in it, the mid doors at its north end, a glass booth and a boom barrier at the south end); A short leaves its north-east corner (an alley turning north onto the site), the customs hall (east door and a window onto mid, west door) leads to the B alley. B tunnel: the transit shed along the west edge (racks down both sides of one aisle). A: the crane berth — an STS crane straddling the quay (bogies 1.5 m: cover nobody climbs), the A box (two containers), a container platform at the back with steps (2.6 m), hatch covers landed on the quay. B: the cold store's loading dock (1.2 m, steps at both ends, two open doors into the cold room with racks), reefers in the yard, a single container and a forklift.
- Walking distances (nav, people's climbs): defenders → A 35 m, → B 37 m; attackers → A 66 m (short) / 77 m (long), → B 68 m (customs) / 81 m (tunnel); both starts → mid 27–29 m; A ↔ B 73 m.
- Geometry: everything between the lanes is stacked containers (yard(): rows along the long side cut into 6 and 12 m boxes, 2–5 levels; the boxes inside a block draw their tops only; faces toward the outside of the map are not drawn). Edges are four-high (north) and three-high stacks, the shed, the cold store and the ship's hull (7.5 m, a 0.5 m strip of water below the quay edge nobody fits into). Scenery: the ship (deck containers, the accommodation block, funnel), a second crane north of A, warehouses outside. Sun from the south-west (MAP.moon as the sun), sky 'skyDay'; seven point lights indoors only. 960 boxes, ~364k vertices (Italy 329k, Q7 402k), 3458 nav nodes; textures ~0.9 s and the rest of the load ~1.5 s on first visit.
- Textures painted on first load: paving, asphalt, the quay apron, road paint, the sea, seven container colours (texContainer), hull and accommodation plating, crane paint, cold-store panels, shed cladding, render, roller shutters, windows, racking, signs (A / B boards, 세관 CUSTOMS, X-RAY INSPECTION, PIER 7) and the bomb-site letters on the ground (cut out, worn, mirrored to read from the south).
- PVP.rndOpen (respawn and 개인전 start spots) only takes nodes a person can walk to from the spawns (PVP.hreach: climbs ≤ 1.45 m): the nav graph also keeps what only a zombie's jump reaches (a crane bogie, a cab roof, the shed's bag pile), and people came back to life up there.
- MAPDEFS.port.bomb holds the two sites (centre, radius), the ways in and the defenders' holding spots for 폭파미션 (v6.50).
- Tests: load and bake, nav reachability of every lane / site / the platform / the dock / the cold room from both starts (the platform's steps first had no foot: a cover block stood against them), 3D views of every area, the title camera, bot TDM (10 players, 120 s: 72 kills, bases 53 m apart) and FFA (8 players), the room window / waiting room map lists per mode, the v6.48 regression run.

## v6.50 — 폭파미션 (bomb.js, built after pvp.js)
- Mode 'bomb': a 대인전 mode (PVP.isMode, G.pvp; PVP.teams() = every mode but 개인전 has BLUE / RED, so the side colours, markers, intel, the kill feed, scoreboard, end card and results follow 팀 데스매치). Only on maps with MAPDEFS[id].bomb (mapOk / mapsFor: the 항만 터미널 so far): A / B (centre, radius), way (the attackers' ways in), hold (the defenders' spots).
- Rounds, no respawns (PVP.kill leaves pvpRT null). RED attacks for the first BOMB.half = W − 1 rounds, then BLUE (BOMB.att()); attackers start at MAP.zspawns, defenders at MAP.spawns. W from the room's pw (CFG.pw / cfg.pw: 4 / 6 / 8, default 6); G.rounds = 2W − 1. A round: 6 s frozen (FREEZE), 1:45 (ROUND), the bomb's 40 s (TIMER) once planted; plant 3.2 s, defuse 6 s (E held; the touch ⚡ button); the end pause 5 s; the buy window runs to 20 s into the fight (pvpBuy). Survivors keep their weapons (ldSnap at the start), the fallen get what they had (ldSnap at death); armour every round; bots change loadouts now and then.
- Wins: the bomb goes off (everyone within 15 m dies, up to 120 damage out to 30 m, a quarter of it taken by armour; the planter gets the kills of enemies, 'c4' in the feed) or every defender is down → attackers; defused, every attacker down before a plant, or the clock out with no plant → defenders. First to W takes the match (PVP.finish: end card, rewards, results; the rewards' "rounds" are the rounds played).
- The bomb (BOMB.b: none / carried / dropped / planted / gone): a random attacker starts with it; a dead carrier drops it where he fell (the host), an attacker who walks over it takes it. Its model (GUNS.c4: four blocks, tape, a timer with a green display, a red light, wires; matGun, probe-lit) rides on the carrier's back (hidden in my own first-person view), lies where it fell, or sits planted with a red light that flashes on each beep (DL) — beeps every 1 s at 40 s left down to 0.1 s at the end (c4beep). Sounds: c4key (plant start), c4def (defuse start), c4arm (planted), c4done (defused); the explosion is FX.explode × 7, a fireball, smoke, a flash, explode at low rate + thunder, shake and muffle near it.
- HUD (BOMB.hud from PVP.hud): the top bar (round N/2W−1, my role in my side's colour, both scores, alive counts), the clock turns into "C4 38.4" (red, blinking) once planted, hints under the crosshair (B window; the carrier's "plant on A or B" / "hold E to plant"; "hold E to defuse"), the plant / defuse bar (#bmBar; red when the defuse would not finish in time), A / B letters over the sites (until a plant) and the bomb (dropped: attackers only; planted: everyone) with its distance (#bmMarks), the radar's site letters, the bomb and a box round my side's carrier (PING.mini wrapper). Round ends: the call ("RED win — Target destroyed", the score); half time: "후반전 — 공수 교대". The scoreboard marks the carrier for his teammates (C4).
- Bots (PVP.bot → BOMB.botAct / BOMB.botGoal): each round the host picks the attackers' site and gives each attacker a way in (alternating: A long / A short, B tunnel / through customs), a 0–5 s wait at the start; the carrier walks to a plant spot inside the site and plants when nobody is in sight, the others take posts round the site facing the defenders' side; after the plant they guard spots 3–9 m from the bomb; the nearest attacker fetches a dropped bomb. Defenders split over A and B (the map's hold spots, facing the ways in), about half go over to the other site when teammates saw attackers there, and all run to a planted bomb and defuse (they fight first while there is time; a defuse under fire goes on when time is short). The defuse kit's clicks bring attackers' bots within 35 m to look.
- Network: the 'round' event carries the carrier (bb); bst (d: dropped at p, c: taken by i, p: planted by i at p on site k), bact (i, k: 0 stop / 1 plant / 2 defuse), bend (winner, why, scores, f: the match is over); the client asks with 'bu' {on} and the host checks it every frame (BOMB.canAct). The snapshot's G.time is the bomb's clock after a plant. Remote players' round-start places are filtered like respawns (pvpSp).
- Lobby: the mode list gains 폭파미션 (modeKey, OPT.mode); the room window and the waiting room show bots, difficulty and 목표 승수 (pw); picking the mode moves the map to one with bomb sites. Room list, invites and the admin page name it. Key help: E (hold).
- Tests (single player only): scripted rounds — a bot plants on A and I defuse it (defenders, 'defuse'), nobody moves ('time'), a bot plants on B, a defender beside it dies and I live at 22 m with 52 health ('bomb', the feed's c4 kills), I plant with E in the second half and kill the defuser (his bar stops), I die carrying it (dropped) and a teammate walks over it (taken); the C4 on a back, on the ground and planted (3D frames); the HUD while planting; full bot matches 3v3 / 4v4 / 5v5 / 6v6 at normal and hard (every way a round can end turns up, plans and plants on both sites, half time, the match end and results, no friendly fire, nobody left standing still); the room window and waiting room rows; the scoreboard, end card and results; the TDM / FFA / mutation regression runs.

## v6.51 — 무기 레이스 (race.js, built after bomb.js)
- Mode 'race': a 대인전 mode played like 개인전 (PVP.teams() is false: a side of one's own, FFA start spots and respawns, 3 s, 2 s of protection). Steps: PVP.GOALS.race = 10 / 15 / 20 (the room's pg, default 15) pick RACE.LADDERS — 15: M4A1, AK-47, SCAR-H, Galil, M249, P90, UMP45, MAC-10, XM1014, M3, AWP, Scout, Desert Eagle, USP, the knife (10 and 20 shorten or lengthen the same run). The clock: PVP.TIMES (5 / 8 / 10 min).
- a.raceLv (0-based) is the step. PVP.kit → RACE.kit: the gun of the step (a fresh magazine and its spare) in its slot, the knife in slot 3, armour; no loadouts, no buying (buyCheck 'na', the B menu and saved sets say why), no dropping (cmd.drop off). A kill (PVP.kill → RACE.onKill): the killer one step up and the next gun at once (RACE.give: the old gun out, equip with its draw, Q goes to the knife); a knife kill also sets the victim back one; a knife kill on the last step wins (PVP.finish after the kill has gone out). A kill made while dead (a level-up for someone down) is collected at the respawn.
- Network: the host works the steps out and sends them with the kill (pk.rl = [killer's step, victim's step]); every page applies them, so a client gives its own player the next gun. PVP.claimOk in the race: the gun of the step, the one before it (a level-up still on its way) or the knife; no grenade claims.
- Order everywhere (leader, MVP, scoreboard, end card, results): step, then kills, then fewer deaths, then score. When the clock runs out the leader wins.
- HUD (RACE.hud from PVP.hud): the top bar (race · N steps, my step vs the leader's — or the runner-up's when I lead — my place and who is first) and #rcBar under it (my step, my gun ▶ the next, a dot per step: mine blue, the leader's gold, the knife's last); notes for a level-up (step and gun; the knife step gets a call), for a knife kill on me (down a step), and when someone else reaches the knife. Scoreboard and results get a step column, the end card shows each step.
- Lobby: OPT.mode gains 무기 레이스; the room window and waiting room show bots, 단계 수 (pg) and the time limit; every map can host it. Room list, invites and the admin page name it.
- Tests (single player): bot races on Q7 (10 steps, 8 players: done in ~2 min by a knife kill), Italy (15 steps, 10 players: ~4.3 min), the port (20 steps, 12 players, hard: ~4.3 min), Big Tree with 4 players and 5 min (the clock ran out at 17/20: the leader won); every living actor always held the gun of his step or the knife; no friendly fire; a level-up by hand (the note, the bar, the gun swap), a knife win; the room window rows, the HUD, scoreboard, end card and results; the TDM / FFA / bomb / mutation regression runs.

## v6.52 — 연막탄 (smoke.js, built last)
- WPN.smoke (slot 4, kind 'nade', model GUNS.smkg: a grey canister with white bands). H_NADES = he · frost · flare · smoke is the one list of human grenades (4 key, wheel, touch bar, HUD 'SMOKE', loadouts LD_NADES, kits); the buy menu's Equipment row, PVP.GUNS, the supply crate refill and the scenario's Full-ammo reward carry it. Once a round like the other grenades.
- The grenade (updNade): fuse 3 s, it pops when it lies still (after 0.35 s) or at the fuse; the canister stays (a thin jet for 6 s) until its cloud is gone. SMK.pop → SMK.add: the cloud's space is built once (SMK.build): a Dijkstra flood over 0.8 m columns from the canister out to R + EDGE/2 (5.95 m) of travel, a column needing a floor (≤ 1.45 m above the canister; a drop or nothing below: it hangs 1.7 m under the canister) and 0.75 m of headroom, a step from one to the next an open line at knee or head height (losClear, so fences and railings let it through) and no climb over 1.2 m; diagonals need both sides open. Each column keeps its ground, ceiling, travel distance d, its wall distance wd (BFS from columns with a blocked side) and a dome top (TOP 3.8 m at the canister, 35 % lower at the rim, under any ceiling).
- Density (SMK.dens): the column's (Re − d) / EDGE (Re grows over GROW 2.4 s), times the height factor (0.9 m under the top, 0.35 m over the ground), times k (0 → 1 over 0.5 s, LIFE 18 s, FADE 3.2 s). SMK.blocks(a, b): the smoke summed along the line in 0.34 m steps (clouds whose 7.4 m bounding sphere the line crosses) ≥ THR 1.6 m → cut. SMK.hides(a, t): eye to chest and eye to head both cut (never within 1.4 m).
- Who it blinds: PVP.botSee and the bots' line of fire; AI.human's zombie sight and its back-off scan; zombie bots' straight run (B.los); the scenario's spitters and giant; RADAR.look; both aim-name readouts (ui.js, pvp.js). ESMK.at / ESMK.blocks (epbase.js) count the clouds too (SMK.atXZ, SMK.blocks2 at each cloud's middle height), so the chapters' bosses lose a human standing in one and bots do not see an epHide zombie through one.
- The look (SMK.draw, every drawn frame after FX): camera-facing puff quads in one mesh (renderOrder 6.5: over FX, tracers and sparks), sorted far to near, a 2×2 atlas of soft blobs (linear filtered), lit at birth from the light probe (smkLit: light grey by day, darker indoors and at night), faded within 1.4 m of the eye. A cloud keeps np = 70 % of its columns (18–104) puffs of radius 1.4–2.1 m, capped by the wall distance, the floor and the ceiling so none pokes through. While it grows each puff rides out along the flood's parent chain (as far as the front Re) to a column of the whole cloud; later ones come up in place, fewer at the rim. The eye inside: a full-screen veil quad (renderOrder 6.6, no depth test: over the world, under the first-person gun) as thick as the smoke at the camera (0.88 at most), lit like the puffs, with drifting noise; inside a cloud puffs nearer than 2 m are not drawn. The radar shows each cloud as a grey patch (SMK.mini, under the pings). Sound 'smoke': a thump and a 3 s hiss.
- Network: every page grows its own cloud from its copy of the grenade (the 'n' fx); the host sends smk {o, p} where it popped there. A client moves its unconfirmed cloud of that thrower there (rebuilt, its puffs replaced) or, when the host's word comes first, makes the cloud and lets its own grenade claim it. clearNades clears the clouds (round starts, the title, the lobby).
- Bots (대인전 only; SMK.botThink at the bots' thinking rate, SMK.botThrow before anything else in PVP.bot): SMK.aim finds yaw and pitch by flying the grenade (SMK.sim, updNade's flight and bounces in 1/120 s steps): a coarse pitch sweep, a finer one round the best, a little to either side; it must come to rest within 3.2 m (one aim a frame across all bots, 1.5 s before the same bot tries again). The throw: equip, turn, fire, hold still until it leaves the hand; given up after 3 s or for an enemy within 32 m (a forced throw goes on). 폭파미션: attackers past their waypoint, 2–32 m short of the site, smoke the site's spots (bomb.smk on the map, else the side toward the defenders' spawn, S.r + 2.5 m out), two a round per site; defenders smoke a planted bomb from 6–26 m (10 s apart), then defuse inside it. Any 대인전 bot under 50 HP in a fight at 12–30 m (half the time, every 5 s at most) smokes the gap and walks back to a spot out of the way for 4 s. PVP.botLd: a smoke 75 % in 폭파미션, 30 % otherwise; a bot's throw puts its gun back up.
- Tests: the cloud in the port's lane and inside the customs hall (no puff through walls or the roof; the grid follows the gate pillar and the hall's doors); a red board behind a cloud at 16 m hidden from 0.5 to 3.5 m; the growth sequence; client/host matching both ways and the host's smk; 10-minute 폭파미션 sims (17–21 smokes thrown, most within 2.5 m of the aim, no stuck bot), TDM and FFA retreat smokes; zombie mode, the scenario and chapters 1, 2, 4 with clouds dropped on the humans every 9 s (no errors); the buy menu, the HUD, the radar patch.

## v6.53 — 대인전 전용맵 「사막 바자르」 (mapbz.js, built after mapport.js)
- MAPDEFS.bazaar: pvp:1 (대인전 modes only), bomb data, bounds −45…45 × −47…40, probe levels .9 / 2.2 / 3.6 / 4.9 / 6.4, the radar split at 2 m. x runs east, z south. A walled town: the town wall (8 m, merlons) west and east, the south wall with three gates (the west gate between two towers, the main gate's arch, the broken east gate with rubble), the kasbah (10 m, two towers, a great gate, lattices) and rows of houses closing the north. Outside the south wall the caravan camp (tents, a truck, crates, pots, palms) where the attackers start; dunes and rock beyond; far sand plateaus, mesas and palms as scenery (coarse lighting cells: bzSandF / bzStoneF, cs 6 / 4).
- Ways: CT forecourt (fountain, palms) → the north lane west to B's north door / the north road east to A's north gate / the arch street south to mid. Mid is the well square (a round well, palms, a stall, lantern wires) with the tea house (its roof a terrace at 3.2 m behind parapets, stairs up from the square; it overlooks the square and the tannery alley). The spice alley leaves the square west and turns north to B's east door; the tannery alley east and north to A's west door. The west gate opens on the carpet street (stalls, a parked car, carpets on lines) up to the B front yard and the hall's arch; the broken east gate on A long (palms along the wall, a trough, a cart) up to the A front yard and the caravanserai's gate.
- A (31.5, −17, r 6.5): the caravanserai — 6.5 m walls, the north gate, the south gate (sign), the west door. The north gallery (roof at 3.6–4 m on four pillars) carries the terrace behind a parapet; stairs up along the east wall from the courtyard (13 steps, a stepped parapet) and, for the defenders, inside the gallery beside the north gate (12 steps along the north wall through a hole in the gallery roof, rails round the hole). Cover: the crates in the middle, a trough, sacks, a cart, a palm.
- B (−31, −16.2, r 6.5): the covered bazaar hall (6.5 m, roof with three skylights, beams, four pillars, lanterns): the south arch (an awning and the BAZAAR sign over it), the east door, the north door in the north-east corner. The mezzanine (3 m) along the north wall with a balustrade (pass: seen and shot through), stairs down the west wall (the attackers' side) and from the north door along the north wall (the defenders'). Cover: the heap of rolled carpets in the middle, stalls, crates, sacks, pots.
- Houses: blk() splits a rectangle into houses of random width (4–8 m), height and plaster colour (four adobe colours), each a solid box with a roof, a parapet, sometimes a water tank (a round prop) and an aerial, windows (grilles, lintels, now and then an awning) and doors only on the faces listed as street faces. Palms: a round bark trunk (deco cylinder), a solid core, a crown of cut-out fronds (four frond textures: along u, along v and the two diagonals). Stalls: a counter (solid, 1 m), posts, a striped canopy and goods (not solid). Textures (painted on first load): sand, sandstone flags, terracotta floor, roofs, four adobe plasters, two dressed stones, planks, four painted doors, the kasbah gate, windows, lattices, balustrades, four awnings, three carpets, glazed tiles, water, fronds, bark, pots, tanks, fruit, sacks, spices, signs (texPtSign / texPtDec from mapport.js) and the sky (dusty blue, a warm haze, the sun low in the west-south-west matching MAP.moon (−.62, .5, .6), thin cloud, mesas and dunes).
- 폭파미션: way A (37, 12) A long / (17.5, −8) the tannery alley, B (−32.5, 12) the carpet street / (−16.5, −8) the spice alley; holds A: the terrace (two), the north gate, the courtyard's corners; B: the mezzanine (two), the west stairs' foot, the north door, the south-east corner, under the mezzanine; smoke spots (bomb.smk, new: SMK.spots reads them) A (31, −23) in front of the terrace and (37.4, −15) the east stairs, B (−31, −20.6) in front of the mezzanine and (−26.4, −23.6) the north door.
- Bots' smoke throws (smoke.js): the flight is now simulated in 1/180 s steps and a landing is taken only when a throw 0.015 rad higher or lower lands within 5 m too (no throws that a bounce off an edge would send astray).
- Tests: stats (1700 boxes, 377k vertices like the port, loads in 1.7 s, 7254 nav nodes, every hold / spot / way / spawn on a node reachable from the starts), paths (CT → A 41 m, CT → B 42, CT → the terrace 48, CT → the mezzanine 43, T → A 69 / B 72 / mid 36), views (every lane, both sites, the terrace, the mezzanine, the tea house, the kasbah, the camp, the overviews, the title orbit), smoke clouds in the hall, TDM (4 min, 106 kills, respawns fine) and 폭파미션 sims (plants on both sites, a defuse, smokes on the map's spots, nobody stuck).

## v6.54 — 좀비모드 신규 맵 「새벽 종합병원 Saebyeok General Hospital」 (mapho_tex.js + mapho.js, built after mapbz.js)
- MAPDEFS.hospital: a zombie-mode map (no pvp flag: Mutation, Original and the PvP modes except 폭파미션), bounds −75…75 × −52…52 (15 600 m², 1.6× the rest stop), dawn: no sun, noStars, skyTex hoSky (navy overhead, pink and gold low in the east-south-east, stratus lit from below, mountains and the city's apartment blocks with their first lit windows), fog #3c4664 / .0125, MAP.moon = the dawn glow (.86, .3, .18) #c8a8b8 × .42. Probe levels .9 / 2.6 / 5.1 / 9.3 / 13.5 / 15.4 (one a storey: 1F, 2F/P2, 3F/P3, the roof), radar cuts 2.6 / 6.8 / 11 (four bands).
- Storeys are 4.2 m (1F 0, 2F 4.2, 3F 8.4, roof 12.6). Wall textures tile with the storey (sv 4.2, vo 0): window bands, handrails and skirting line up on every floor. hoFacade (outside: white panels, four windows per 8.4 m, blinds), hoWallWin (the inside of an outer wall: the same four windows, the dawn city in them), hoWall / hoWallB / hoWallC (mint, beige, blue-grey: a wooden handrail at 0.9 m, a vinyl wainscot), hoTile (theatres). cs 1–2 m on the big faces keeps it at ~440k vertices.
- The main building x −32…32, z −38…−8. deck(x0,z0,x1,z1,y,floor,zones,holes) lays a slab with a floor per zone (later zones win) and holes (the atrium void x −10…10 × z −28…−16, the stairwells' flights). grid(y,…) builds the same rooms on every floor: north rooms (z −37.6…−31), corridor N (−31…−28), middle rooms (−28…−22), corridor S (−22…−19), south rooms (−19…−8.4); per wing a cross corridor at the far end (x ±28.6…±31.6): every wing is a racetrack. pX / pZ: 0.2 m partitions a storey high with doors [u0,u1], [u0,u1,h] or hatches/windows [u0,u1,y0,y1].
- wallX / wallZ cannot stack openings in one column (the first hole's lintel runs to the top, the next one's sill from the bottom): walls with a door on several floors (the stairwells, the east wall's ER doors) are built a storey at a time.
- stairwell(x0,z0,levels,decals,wall): 3.4 × 6.6 m, door side south. Per storey a flight of 8 (0.2625 m risers, 0.4 m treads: the 1 m nav grid never sees more than 0.79 m between neighbours) up north in the west lane, a half landing, 8 back south in the east lane; a wall between the lanes; the first flight's east lane is filled under; the walls rise 3 m over the top level (the hut on the roof), the decals (1F…RF, P1…P3) on each landing. Lifts: a solid block with door decals.
- 1F: the lobby (granite) under the three-storey atrium (skylight hoSkyG, pass: light and sight through), reception, the floor guide, seats, a ficus, kiosks, vending; the escalator 1F→2F (16 steps, stepped glass balustrades) up the west side to the north gallery, 2F→3F across the void from the south gallery. West: linen store, administration (a service window onto corridor S), records, the convenience store (shelves, a fridge wall, a street door), the pharmacy (its counter along the lobby). East: X-ray, blood draw, the lab, the outpatient waiting room, a clinic, the door into the emergency wing. Behind the lobby: the stores and the dock's two roll-up doors.
- 2F: the ICU behind a glass wall (six beds), radiology (CT gantry, control, reading, waiting, MRI) with the skybridge door, clinics, the staff lounge, the lounge over the entrance with the door out onto the canopy. 3F: the ward (301 four beds, 303 two, the nurse station with a hatch onto corridor S, 305 six, 306 four; a bed barricade in corridor N with a gap), the operating suite behind steel double doors at x 13 (theatres 1–3, scrub, recovery), the conference room. Roof: the helipad (raised 0.4 m, its H, edge lights, the helicopter), air handlers, tanks, solar panels, the aerial, the big sign facing the plaza.
- Outside: the emergency wing x 32…58 (resuscitation, three isolation rooms behind glass, bays with curtains, the nurse station island, triage), its roof a terrace level with 2F (skylights, planters, condensers, a tank); the canopy over the ambulance bay; one ambulance stands half out from under it so zombies jump from its roof (2.7) onto the canopy (4.2: 1.5 m, beyond people's 1.45). The parking tower x −72…−42 (P1 0, P2 4.2, P3 8.4): ramp 1 (21 steps of 0.2) up north on the west lane, ramp 2 up south on the east lane, a wall between, stepped side walls, slots railed; a stairwell in the north-east corner; carY() = car() on a deck. The skybridge (glass walls, pass) at 2F over the service road. The plaza: the canopy, the loop round the fountain island (the sunrise sculpture, the name on a low wall), the bus stop; the car park (stalls, a burnt car); the checkpoint (two tents, sandbags, barriers, the truck, floodlights, the burning car at the shut gate: MAP.fires, MAP.smoke); the healing garden (pond, pergola, pavilion on a 0.8 m floor); the service yard (the dock and a box truck, the oxygen tank in its fence, generators, bins). Brick walls 3.2 m with a fence on top (hoFence, coarse); the city outside (hoApt blocks, shops).
- Spawns: 16 in the lobby; 27 zombie entries over every level (yard, garden, car park, P1–P3, ER, every corridor, the roof, the ER roof): the zombie modes draw everyone's start from both lists. 18 camps (helipad, roof west, theatres, ward, nurse station, ICU, CT, canopy, ER roof, ER station, pharmacy, store, P3, P2, skybridge, pavilion, dock, tents).
- map.js: the round props' light (drums, trees, tanks) is cached per corner (the cylinders are not indexed: each corner was lit up to six times): every map's load is shorter, the result is the same.
- Tests: stats (2990 boxes, 438k vertices, 173 lights, 19.3k nav nodes, loads in ~4.7 s here (rest stop 2.7)), every area reachable from the lobby by people and zombies (an unpruned flood: no sealed floor left), every spawn / entry / camp on a reachable node, the zombie shortcut (bay → ER roof 30 m for zombies, 84 for people), views of every area, the radar's four bands; sims: Mutation 15 bots (round 1 the zombies win with 31 s left, nobody stuck, ~7 ms a frame), Original 15 bots (2:2 over four rounds), Mutation 7 bots, TDM / FFA / Arms Race (kills, nobody standing still, no errors). Multiplayer not tested here: the owner tests it.

## v6.55 — JANUS-2 (janus2.js, built right after janus.js)
- Made to the owner's design sheet (a JSON from the gun-design prompt, its id written as janus1, renamed janus2): parts, points, colours, numbers, motions, sounds as written. Changes kept small and named in janus2.js's head: the figure of Christ (12 × 18 × 10 mm) and its eyes moved 7 mm back so they stand out of the sight's back face (the sheet buried them in it, the face flush with the back: z-fighting), a 5 cm bridge under the sight (it floated 2.5 cm over the frame), the inner gold barrel 162 mm (sheet 160) with a bore so its mouth no longer shares the shroud's face. Mary (only named in the sheet) is ours: blue veil, face, robe, folded hands, lit eyes, seven gold rays.
- Atlas: j2Blue, j2Gold (brushed, the sheet's three tones), j2Green (grain), j2Skin, j2Glow (self-lit, 232); janus_engraved is realguns.js's deGold. The atlas is full now: 128 of 128 slots (GA 1024 × 2048, 128 px). The next material needs more rows (GA.H 4096 keeps it a power of two) and every slot check (GA_SLOTS) follows from GA.H.
- FS_VM: a texel with alpha under .97 (≤ 247) is drawn at least at its own colour (self-lit) in the first-person view, whatever realguns.js's comment says about 240…254: a sheen that should not glow in the view model needs 248…254 (FS_CHAR reads (a − 239) / 15 as gloss on world models). j2Blue 248, j2Gold 251, the rest matte.
- First-person model (GUNS.janus2): tag 'mag' = what breaks (inner barrel, bore, bridge, sight, its gold strip) about G.hinge [0,.02,−.07] (the sheet's pivot); the outer barrel, the engraved strip under it and the rail are halved into j2L / j2R; four thorns thR1/2, thL1/2 (pivot = their centre); j2J (Christ), j2E (eyes), j2M (Mary), shell. j2Rig (a buildVM hook) hangs all of those under the 'mag' group (their positions made relative to it) so they turn with the break and still move on their own in the form; base positions are taken after that (VM.set). World models janus2n (= janus2s) / janus2b (the form baked: halves ±1 cm, thorns turned +0.5 rad about z through their centre with j2Turn — a quaternion so a thorn's own rx survives — Mary for Christ).
- One-handed (the sheet's sup / leftHand null): buildVM hides armL for a gun with no sup; j2Rig gives it a rest pose out of sight below, VMX.janus2 shows it only while a clip with a hand track runs (the reload). FDS.break and rlBrk are wrapped: a gun with no sup gets fdBreak1 (thumb latch, the barrel drops 45°, rolled over for a look (roll), a flick of the wrist shuts it on the 'mag' spring with the gun's shut sound) and rlBrk1 (the same, plus the extractor throwing the case back along the bore — 'shell' pz / py / rx / s keys — the free hand bringing a grenade (FD_PROPS.j2nade) from the belt to the chamber's mouth riding the barrel, the case sliding back in). GUN_DESIGN.janus2 carries the sheet's first draw plus open .785 and its sounds (opS / shut).
- janus.js generalised: JNW[id] holds what differs between JANUS weapons (the form's round wb, sOpen / sOn / sClose, the open / close clips, burst, the light lit / spark, the view model's glow mz, the crosshair class cx, bfx / sfx / cfx); WPN[id].jn = the id ('janus1' now a string; anything that tested it for truth still works). JNP[kind] = whose grenade, speed, model (jnProj builds any of them, the nyProj hook takes any JNP kind). jnHeld(a) replaces a.cur === 'janus1'; a.jnLeft is per id ([mag, spares]); a.jnWid = the JANUS weapon the actor's charge belongs to — another one in the pistol slot clears it (and shuts a form). The bot opener waits out a reload.
- Ammo: res 0 as the sheet says. A supply crate (SUP.give hook) gives back what is missing as spares (never past 5 in all), an ammo box (EPK.give) half of the magazine, the range tops it up; the reload (W.brk, 1 s) loads them. The buy menu's ammo / buying it again do nothing (supOnly, as JANUS-1); the chapter's 탄약 가득 reward and the resets fill it directly.
- Blast: detonate 'gl' takes n.sp, the share left at the edge (default .35 = the old .35 + .65 f): JANUS-2 .5 (the sheet's splash ×0.5) with dmg 80 (× 2.5 in the chapters, as JANUS-1), R 4.4, kb .5, stag .35, up .8. The sheet's "human 20" has nothing to hit (no friendly fire, the JANUS guns are not in PVP.GUNS).
- HUD: the jn / jnb crosshair and the ring gain the weapon's cx class: j2 = gold charge and signal, the form #8A2BE2. Sounds j2fire (deep 펑 + crack, a 0.8 s tail), j2fireb, j2open (철깍), j2shut (텅), j2draw (웅- + 철컥, W.drawS), j2awake (우웅-, the form opening), j2end; jnsig / jncyc shared.
- Shop: sidearm decoder only (SHOP_NOTSOLD, gun_prices janus2 0 / not sold); side_lines swaps one coins:1000 for gun:janus2 — schema.sql changes the column default, the config row if it is still the v6.36 list, and every open card's first coins:1000 line not done yet (the v6.13 Salamander pattern, placed after side_boards is created). Checked on a local Postgres 16: an old database with an open card (one coins:1000 line done) → the other one became gun:janus2, the config and a new card carry it, a second run changes nothing; a fresh database too.
- Tests: the model from every side (normal and form), the view model's idle, first draw, reload (case out, hand in, flick), the form opening / on / closing, third person in hand; unit (blast 80 → 40.9 at 4.3 m, nothing at 4.5; five counted blasts → signal; the form 8 shots in 2 s, nothing spent; 5 then dry; supply → spares → reload 5/0; ammo box +3; buy menu ammo full / in hand own; taken again 2/1; JANUS-1 beside it: charge, form, its own rounds, the crosshair without j2); bot sims Mutation 240 s / Original 200 s with JANUS-2 (and JANUS-1) in every hand: no errors; pvp/reg.py.

## v6.56 — three crate weapons of our own: SENTINEL · Q7 VACCINE LAUNCHER · BLOODHOUND (sentinel.js, q7vac.js, bhound.js, built after janus2.js)
- The owner asked for original supply-crate weapons "like Event Horizon", each with a clear idea and a clear merit; of the concepts offered he kept 1, 2 and 4: a machine gun that turns into a sentry, a vaccine that cures zombies back to the human side, a sniper rifle that marks. All three are crate guns (WPN.crate, CRATE_GUNS, the buy-menu category for an account that owns one, lent in the range, never in 대인전), SHOP_NOTSOLD and gun_prices rows (0, not sold) like ak60r / dmp7. Supply crate: Event Horizon 30 %, crate guns 35 % (from 30), heavy 15 % (from 20), kit 20 %.
- Atlas full (128 / 128): only existing materials. The view model's FS_VM reads unsaturated light texels as bare metal (a sheen, then the flashlight) — 'white' on a big surface blows out in first person, so the vaccine gun's body is sl_medic (pale teal) and its stock mtP; white stays on the vial's cap.
- SENTINEL (sentinel.js): WPN 150 / 300, 33 × 720/min, belt reload (rlKind hook → 'belt', GUN_DESIGN act belt). Right click (W.alt 'sentry', nyAlt hook) → sntDeploy: a spot ahead (sntSpot: floor within ±0.6 m, room for it), a.stOut = true, the gun stays in inv[1] (drops, loadouts, round keep and crates work as for any gun), the sidearm comes out (bestWeapon skips a sentry that is out). The sentry (SNT.list): GUNS.sntBase (tripod, turntable, battery box) + sntHead (the gun without folded legs) on a yaw group (SNN.H) and a pitch group (pivot SNT_PIV = the box's bottom), red eye sntEyeR while on a target. SNN: 30 a round (×2 head), 600/min, kb 3.4, stag .25, 24 m, turns 5 / 3 rad/s, fires within .14 rad, 45 s watch, .7 s to set up, .55 to fold. It feeds on a.ammo.sentinel (box, then spares; a supply kit refills it while it stands).
- Calling back: equip(sentinel) while it is out (1, the wheel, Q) → sntRecall (folds, then equips); a bot's own wish to switch back is ignored (its sentry returns by itself and the bot takes it then). It folds and returns on empty / after the watch; it is removed with its owner (fallen, turned, gone) or when inv[1] changes; NY.clear removes all. A page that takes the owner over (host moved, a player became a bot) puts the gun back in his hands.
- Network: the owner's page aims and deals the damage (sntShot → damageActor: a client's hits are claims as any shot); every other page runs the same aiming as a ghost (look and sound, NET.ghost) from ['st', id, 1, x, y, z, yaw] / ['st', id, 0] (netFxPush; relayed by the host as the actor's own fx). HUD: .sntMk over the player's own sentry (rounds · seconds; red while on a target). Bots: a bot holding it sets it down when its seen zombie is 5–26 m away (4 s apart). Sounds sentfire (gunshot GUNDEF.sentinel), sntdep, sntfold, sntlock, sntempty.
- Q7 VACCINE LAUNCHER (q7vac.js): WPN 5 / 15 darts, 90 (×2 head), 66/min semi with a pump stroke (W.pump; GUN_DESIGN act pump, rl mag — the vial is the 'mag' tag), hitscan. The host / solo decides in a damageActor hook: a q7vac hit on a zombie that vaxCurable (Original / Mutation only, not a host, not reviving, hp ≤ 50 %) → vaxCure instead of damage; otherwise the damage and the burn (vaxSlow: maxSpeed × .55 for 2 s, netEv 'vxs' so a player zombie's own page slows too).
- vaxCure → vaxHuman (every page; clients through CLIH.cure under netWithEv): team TH, maxHp 100, hp 60, no armour, everything zombie cleared, inv = the loadout's own sidearm and melee (a.ldo[2] / [3] when they are plain guns of that slot, else p9 / knife) so the next round's loadout (ldCur falls back to a.ldo for an empty slot) is kept; setHull, ensureRig, equip; the player's VM and the infection tint; AI.onTeam; immunity a.vaxImm = 4 s (an infect hook ignores the host's own infections meanwhile). The curer: +3 score, scEarn 400, a.cures; Mutation morale +2 points (as a kill). Feed: a row of its own (the name in human colour) with a green cross (HUD.feed / renderFeed hooks; MINI.vax).
- The bio-scanner (vaxHud): while the player holds it in an infection mode, up to six zombies in sight within 40 m get .vaxMk: health %, '+ 치료' (green) when a dart would cure, '면역' on a host. FX: green burst / ring / light on a cure, green drips on a slowed zombie, a shimmer on the immune. Sounds vaxfire (valve hiss, thump, pump), vaxcure (chime over a chord), vaxhit.
- BLOODHOUND (bhound.js): WPN 5 / 20, 105 (×3 head), 50/min bolt, zoom 28 / 10 like the AWP, GUN_DESIGN act bolt. A damageActor hook on the host: a hit by it marks (hndMark: t.hndT = 10 s, t.hndBy, netEv 'hnd'); a marked zombie takes ×1.5 from any human (×1.25 a boss: ZBIG or the chapter's boss). killZombie hook: brought down by someone else while marked → the hunter's assist (+1 score, a.assists). Every human page: .hndMk (◆ distance, blinking in its last 2 s) over marked zombies whether or not they are in sight, RADAR.aware true for them, a red tint (updateVisual hook, over no other tint). A marked player zombie gets a note. NY.clear and a cure clear the marks. Sounds bhfire (GUNDEF.bhound), bhmark (sonar ping).
- Tests: models from every side, the view models (idle, the belt reload, the pump, the vial reload), the sentry set up in the world; unit — sentry: deploy, 24 hits in 3 s off the gun's rounds, recall with the rounds left, empty fold, the 45 s watch, removed on a new primary / death, right click, a kit while it stands, NY.clear, a bot deploying and not recalling; vaccine (Mutation and Original): 90 and the slow (4.75 → 2.61), the cure (human, 60 / 100, p9 + its own melee, 3.8 s immunity, feed row with the cross), immunity then infection after 4 s, a host only hurt, the scanner marks, a player zombie cured by a bot, no cure in the scenario; Bloodhound: 105 and a 10 s mark, a teammate's 100 → 150, radar and screen mark, gone after 10 s, the assist, a boss 125, NY.clear. Sims 420 s Mutation / Original with 12 bots carrying the three (sentries 11–12, cures 3, marks 24–36, assists, no errors); JANUS sim; reg.py (대인전 lists without them, footer v6.56); crate odds over 10 000 opens; schema on local Postgres 16 (old → new and fresh).

## v6.85 — the zombie pain sound, better: stacked voices, oversampled, 48 kHz, four variants (tools/zombie_pain.py v3; sound/zombie_pain_1 … _4 replace zombie_pain; audio.js: a sound can have recorded variants; design.js)
- The owner's word, after hearing v6.84: 「사운드 퀄리티 더 높혀줘」. Measured against the old file, v6.84's spectrum moved less than the voice's (its frame-to-frame change below 2 kHz 2.8 dB against 3.6), its glottal clicks sat on whole samples, the saturation ran at 44.1 kHz (what it adds above half the rate folds back as grit), its top was a brick-wall 8th-order low-pass down to 1.3 kHz (copying the old file's lossy encoder, which made the end muffled), and every hit played the same file.
