- The new voice (tools/zombie_pain.py): glottal flow pulses (rising 47 % of the period, a fast close) placed at their exact times and drawn at 4x the rate, each pulse's opening jittered (its tilt moves pulse by pulse), the pitch 178 → 264 → 294 Hz with a small tremor and wander, period jitter 17 %, pulse jitter 36 %, every other pulse 38 % weaker (the growl); breath noise on the open phase, flapping at 33 Hz (a throat rattle); four formant resonators moving from "aah" to "uh" (coefficients every 16 samples, each wandering a little) over a chest resonance. Three such voices stacked (the second 0.85 loud, 3.8 % lower; the third 0.54, a little higher; each its own randomness): a thick throat instead of one buzzing oscillator. A breathy onset and a few wet blips. Then at 4x the rate: a slightly lopsided saturation (even harmonics: warmth), the envelope, a gentle limit, a 24 dB/oct low-pass closing from 6730 to 1200 Hz; down to 48 kHz through the polyphase filter; a low shelf +4.2 dB at 285 Hz and a high shelf -9.6 dB at 5889 Hz (body below, the top kept clean but not hissy); TPDF-dithered to 16 bits; ogg q8 and mp3 192 kb/s (11–17 KB each).
- The numbers: the same search as v6.84 (loudness, mel spectral shape — the top above 5 kHz weighed lightly, the old file's top being its encoder's —, brightness, roughness, six octaves, envelope modulation) plus how much the spectrum moves below 2 kHz; nothing of the old recording goes in. Variant 1 against the old file: loudness every 20 ms within 1.2 dB (the loud part the same, +0.1 dB), brightness within 0.14 octave, roughness the same, envelope modulation as close as v6.84's, octaves 6.5 / 16.5 / 54.9 / 16.0 / 5.0 / 1.1 % against 6.6 / 13.6 / 57.9 / 16.0 / 4.6 / 1.3 %, movement below 2 kHz 3.0 dB (v6.84 2.8, the old file 3.6); clean above 7 kHz at -39 dB (v6.84 -50, the old file -43, cut by its encoder).
- Four variants (seed, pitch ×, formants ×, length ×): (7, 1, 1, 1), (11, 0.97, 0.99, 1.03), (13, 1.03, 1.02, 0.97), (17, 0.985, 1, 0.95) — 0.60 to 0.65 s; with the game's own ±5 % pitch on each play no two hits in a row are alike.
- audio.js: SOUND_DESIGN[name].file may be a list of lists — variants, each with its formats ([[ogg, mp3], …]); all are loaded into the bank and one is picked at random each time (as the synthesised sounds' variants). One list (formats of one sound) and a single path work as before. design.js: zpain → the four, the header explains the form.
- Tests: in the game the bank holds four buffers from the files (48 kHz mono, each its length), plays pick among them, an unreadable ogg falls back to its mp3, a single-file sound (v6.84's form) still loads; plays in a running context pick among the four, AU.at too; each file decodes back to its synthesis (correlation 0.9995 ogg, 1.000 mp3); the tool run again gives the same sounds sample for sample.

## v6.84 — the zombie pain sound made from scratch (sound/zombie_pain.ogg / .mp3 new, Zombi_hurt_01 gone; tools/zombie_pain.py new; design.js)
- The owner's word: 「현재 좀비가 맞을때 나는 소리를 최대한 비슷하게 대체할만한 비슷한 사운드를 만들어줘 — 직접 대조해보면서 비슷하게 하나 새로」. zpain (every hit on a zombie, 0.35 s apart per zombie; the bosses' stagger and fall at a lower rate) played sound/Zombi_hurt_01 since v6.1.3, a file from the owner's sound folder.
- tools/zombie_pain.py makes the new one from numbers alone (numpy / scipy, ffmpeg for the ogg and the mp3): glottal pulses at a pitch rising for a moment and falling (147 → 230 → 160 Hz), each period and pulse jittered and every other pulse weaker (a growl), breath noise on the pulses; four formant resonators moving from an open "aah" to a closed "uh" (F1 757 → 250, F2 911 → 925, F3 2657 → 2363, F4 3704 Hz) over a low chest resonance; a short breathy onset; tanh saturation (a dense, loud grunt, as the old one); a steep 8th-order low-pass closing as the voice fades; a 0.63 s envelope (44 ms up, held 306 ms, a 300 ms tail). Mono 44.1 kHz; ogg q5 and mp3 128 kb/s.
- How its numbers were found: the old file was only measured — its loudness every 20 ms, its spectral envelope in 32 mel bands every 20 ms, its spectral centroid and YIN aperiodicity every 20 ms, its energy in six octaves, the modulation spectrum of its 300–2000 Hz envelope — and a random search (two noise seeds each try) moved the synthesis numbers to the closest measures. Nothing of the recording is in the new sound. Result: the share of energy in the six octaves 100–6400 Hz 6.4 / 13.9 / 57.7 / 16.1 / 4.6 / 1.3 % against the old 6.6 / 13.6 / 57.9 / 16.0 / 4.6 / 1.3 %; the loudness every 20 ms within 1.3 dB on average (the loud part 0.6 dB quieter: no hard clipping); the brightness (spectral centroid, 1200 Hz falling to about 400) within 0.09 octave frame by frame; the roughness (YIN aperiodicity, about 0.55) the same on average; the envelope's modulation 2–8 / 8–20 / 20–50 / 50–120 / 120–200 Hz 5.6 / 11.3 / 14.1 / 37.6 / 31.1 % against 5.0 / 7.4 / 14.5 / 40.3 / 31.6 %. Where it differs: the old one's top edge is a lossy encoder's staircase, this one falls off smoothly.
- design.js: zpain → sound/zombie_pain.ogg (mp3 for the iPhone). The old files are deleted from source/sound and the site's sound/.
- Tests: both files decode back to the synthesis (correlation 0.995 ogg, 1.000 mp3); in the game the bank loads zombie_pain.ogg (mono 44.1 kHz, from the file), an unreadable ogg falls back to the mp3, the old name answers 404, a hit plays it with no error or CSP report; re-running the tool gives the same sound.

## v6.83 — the global chat keeps 12 hours (gchat.js; schema.sql chat_lines, qz_chat_post, qz_chat_recent; the legal pages; lobby.js notes)
- The owner's word: 「로비 공동 채팅은 12시간 이후에 자동삭제 — 누군가 보내놔도 새로 접속한 유저가 그 채팅은 12시간이 지나기 전까진 보이게」. Before (v6.75): nothing was kept; a line reached only the pages connected at that moment.
- schema.sql: chat_lines (id, user_id → auth.users on delete cascade, kid, ts, t, s, r, at; unique (kid, ts); RLS on, no grants: only the two functions touch it). qz_chat_post(p_kid, p_ts, p_t, p_s, p_r): signed in, not suspended (qz_guard), the chat on; 1–120 characters without control characters; the line's time within 10 minutes of the server's; a signature-shaped s; the key must be this player's own (chat_keys) — so the server vouches for who wrote a kept line; a room card checked (code, mode, map, 1 ≤ players ≤ seats ≤ 8) and rebuilt from those five fields; the profile row locked, then 0.7 s apart, five in 14 s, a room card every 25 s (the page asks 1.5 s / 15 s / 30 s: a late packet is not refused); then every post deletes lines older than 12 hours and all but the newest 2,999 before it goes in (on conflict (kid, ts) do nothing). qz_chat_recent(): the newest 100 of the last 12 hours, oldest first, as {k, ts, t, r, n: the nickname now, tt / ft: the titles as qz_chat_who gives them, me}; none from a suspended account or one I blocked; [] while the chat is off. Both for authenticated only.
- Size: at most 3,000 rows of a few hundred bytes (about 1 MB) — and in practice only what half a day of chat holds; a deleted account's lines go with it.
- gchat.js: a line sent goes on the channel as before (signed, live) and to qz_chat_post (keepLine; a refusal or a server before v6.83 is quiet — it still reached everyone online; chat_off switches the chat off as elsewhere). Every join of the channel loads qz_chat_recent (history): items checked (key, time inside 12 hours, text, room card), deduplicated against what the page has seen (kid:ts), blocked names left out, merged by time under the live lines, the last KEEP (100) kept; no unread dot for them. Kept lines are not checked against a key (an account keeps only its three newest keys, so an older line's key may be gone): the server vouches for them. The page lets go of lines past 12 hours (tick). A line from before today shows 어제 HH:MM. The note under the box: 채팅은 12시간 동안 남아서 나중에 들어온 사람도 볼 수 있어요 (short: 12시간 보관).
- test/mock.js (the stand-in server the tests use): qz_chat_post / qz_chat_recent with the same rules (db.clines); window.__MOCK_NOCHATLOG = a database before v6.83.
- The legal pages (effective date 2026-10-19): privacy — what is collected (전체 채팅에 쓴 메시지, 12시간 보관), what others see, the note on chats, the retention table (전체 채팅: 보낸 뒤 12시간, 탈퇴하면 바로); terms — room chat is not kept, the lobby's global chat 12 hours. lobby.js: the v6.75 news line loses its 「내용은 저장 안 돼요」.
- Tests: schema on a local PostgreSQL 16 with the Supabase stubs (fresh, re-run and on top of v6.82's): every refusal (empty, 121 characters, a control character, 11 minutes off, a bad signature, another player's key, room cards 9/8 · lower-case code · 2.5 players, 0.7 s, the sixth in 14 s, suspended, chat off), a resent line kept once, the room card rebuilt without extra fields, a newcomer reading 8 lines with the sender's nickname, mine marked, blocked and suspended left out, off → [], anon and the table itself refused, a 13-hour line not read and deleted by the next post, 3,208 lines → 3,000 after a post, the read 100 oldest first, a deleted account's lines gone. The game with the mock server (1280×720, 390×844): two lines kept; another account on the same page sees them under the first one's name; a line of its own not doubled after a re-join; a line made 13 hours old not loaded, one past 12 hours on the page dropped; 어제 23:50; a server without the functions: no history, the chat still live.

## v6.82 — joining a match under way (latejoin.js new, built last; net.js protocol r12, NET_LATE, the room list; quick.js; lobby.js notes; style.css; no server change)
- The owner's 3번 of the list after v6.81 (게임 중인 방 중간 참가). Before: HOSTH.hello sent 'bye' ingame to anyone new once a match had started (「이미 게임이 진행 중인 방이에요」), so 빠른 매칭 passed over every playing room and the list could only say 게임중.
- net.js: NET_VER ends -r12 (a page of v6.81 or older and one of this version never meet: an older host would refuse, an older client would not know 'late' / 'jn'). NET_LATE = mut · orig · tdm · ffa · race · bomb (not scen: its pool of zombie actors, the chapter's state and the hall of fame's team are made at the start). The room list: a playing room in NET_LATE with a seat gets .late and 「중간참가」 (title: 바로, 또는 관전하다가 다음 라운드부터); others still 게임중.
- latejoin.js, the host (HOSTH.hello wrapped: a game on, the same version, no hand-over hello, a key without an actor): LATE.why() — a hand-over, a mode not in NET_LATE, the match on its last result card (infection: the last round or a side past half; 대인전: the end; 폭파미션: BOMB.final) → 'ingame'; 8 players or 24 actors → 'full'. Else LATE.admit: the waiting-room entry (the hello's fields cleaned as before) into NET.lob.pl (so the order of succession has them); an actor with the next id (LATE.mk = startMatch's own setup), a side (tdm: the smaller team counting bots; ffa / race: id + 2), a spawn; to the others 'jn' {r: roster row, s: side, p: place, a: in now}; to the newcomer 'start' {cfg (without ro), ro: every actor, late: LATE.state} and L.inGame; the room's list a moment later (netLobbyCast after 150 ms: its own message).
- When they play: mut / orig in the preparation with more than 2 s left → LATE.live at once (a round start's fresh human); else held (lateW, not alive, no a.r0) until the next round's plan places them (startRound wrapped: lateW off for the living). tdm / ffa / race: in the warm-up PVP.respawn at once, in the fight a.pvpRT = 1.5 (the host's usual respawn, 'prs'); bomb: in its freeze (more than 2 s) PVP.respawn at BOMB.spots()' place for their side, else the next round. PVP.respawn wrapped: lateW off.
- late (LATE.state): st, clock, round, score, host count, morale, the blackout's time left, the end-card timer; per actor [id, netFlags, zombie class, health, armour, max health, level, side, race step, x, y, z, yaw, weapon, waiting, the round's start kills / infections / damage or -1 for none] — the snapshots fill health and places from then on and S the scoreboard every half second; 폭파미션 the bomb (state, carrier, place, site, final); supply boxes [id, place, t] and dropped guns [id, gun, place, yaw, t, at rest] — not over the claude.ai room relay, where a message must fit a 4 KiB presence.
- The newcomer (CLIH.start wrapped: after netBegin builds the actors from the roster, LATE.apply): state and clock; each actor's side / race step / place; the waiting stay down; the own player alive and human → live; zombies TZ with their class, claw and rig; the dead drawn down; the bomb, the boxes (landed ones without the chute, quietly: the drop's call and sound held off) and the guns (at rest lying flat); BO.start for a blackout still on. The note: 진행 중인 방에 들어왔어요 (· 클릭해서 시작 on a desktop).
- Waiting (LATE.wait): #lateBan just under the HUD's round / score / head count (its top from #hTop's bottom every 300 ms): 관전 중 — 다음 라운드부터 함께해요 · <name> 보는 중 · 클릭 (touch: 사격 버튼): 다른 사람 — or 곧 투입돼요… for 대인전's respawn; the usual 부활할 수 없습니다 line (#hSpec) is hidden meanwhile (html.ljw). Spectating is the dead player's camera (Main.specOn: down, human, death cam over). In again (a round, or a respawn — at once for the respawn): the line goes; 대인전 but 무기 레이스 asks for loadout set 1 as a match start does (PVP.autoSet).
- A round only watched claims nothing (shopRoundReward needs a.r0; ui.js' round card and the MVP pick skip actors without one).
- 빠른 매칭: QM.ok takes a playing room in NET_LATE (with a seat, a known map); QM.pick scores it 30 lower (a waiting room with 3 fewer players comes first); in = NET.ui lobby or game; the note on the HUD when in a match.
- Tests (single pages with stub links, no real multiplayer): the host in 뮤테이션 — joined in the preparation (alive, 'jn' a=1 to the player already in, start > lobby), in the fight (waiting, no r0, the start 915 bytes with 7 actors), the next round places and frees them; refusals: full (no actor, no start), the scenario, the last result card, a hand-over, another version (ver); 팀 데스매치 — the warm-up (start > prs, side 0), the fight (1.5 s later alive, sides 4 : 4); 개인전 side id + 2; 폭파미션 — the freeze (start > prs), the fight (waiting, the bomb's carrier in late). The newcomer's page (844×390 and 1280×720): the line and whom it watches, #hSpec hidden, the zombies with rigs, 사격 버튼 / click to the next, the next round's plan → in and the line gone, another 'jn'; a box and a gun from late; 팀 데스매치's 곧 투입돼요 → 'prs' → in, two buy asks (set 1); 폭파미션's bomb carried by the host's carrier. The list (1280×720, 390×844): 중간참가 on one line for mut / tdm / bomb rooms under way, 게임중 for the scenario and a full room; quick match: a waiting room with 1 before a match under way with 3, a match with 5 before it, joined into ui game with the note on the HUD.

## v6.81 — the game as an app: 홈 화면에 추가, a service worker, the new-version banner (pwa.js new, built last; pwa/ new: sw.js, manifest.webmanifest, icons/; build.py; vercel.json; style.css; no server change)
- The owner's 4번 of the list after v6.78. On an iPhone the browser's bars take the screen and Safari has no fullscreen for a page (FS.ok() is false there); added to the home screen the game opens on its own, full screen, under its own icon. Android needs a manifest, a service worker and icons for Chrome to offer an install.
- pwa/ (copied by build.py into dist/standalone next to the page; at the repo root with the pages): manifest.webmanifest (id and start_url /, display fullscreen with standalone as the fallback, orientation any — the lobby has both shapes, the theme #0e0f12, three icons), icons/ (icon-192, icon-512, icon-maskable-512 with the face inside the 80 % safe zone, apple-touch-icon 180 — drawn once from the game's own art: the rager's head from portrait(), the lobby's gold Z in Galmuri11, the quarantine tape), sw.js. version.json is written by the build: {v: the patch notes' newest, b: the build id}.
- build.py: BID = the first 12 hex of sha-256(js + style.css + body.html), put into pwa.js's '%BUILD_ID%'; the standalone head gets the manifest link, theme-color, the 192 icon (no more /favicon.ico 404), the apple-touch-icon and apple-mobile-web-app-title (relative links: a file opened from disk just finds nothing); the CSP's worker-src is 'self' blob: (was blob: — the worker comes from the site). vercel.json: the manifest served as application/manifest+json.
- sw.js (scope /): install puts '/' in the cache qz-page-1 (so the first visit already works offline) and skips waiting; activate drops other caches and claims the pages. fetch: only the page's own navigations ('/' and '/index.html' on the site's origin — the navigation request itself is fetched, so the cleanUrls redirect of /index.html is the browser's to follow); everything else (the account server, PeerJS, the sounds, the manifest and icons, /admin /privacy /terms) goes as without a worker, and where the browser has static routes (install: addRoutes {not: requestMode navigate} → network) without even waking it — a worker woken for every request had made the x2 test's injected image error arrive later. Network first: the answer is kept when it is a plain 200 and its ETag changed (or there is none); the network failing → the kept copy. Online a new version is there at once (Vercel's max-age=0, must-revalidate makes the browser ask every time; a 304 is quick).
- pwa.js (only on the site: https, or http on localhost for tests; never in the claude.ai artifact's iframe or a file opened from disk): registers /sw.js; keeps beforeinstallprompt (preventDefault) for 앱 설치; on iOS (iPhone / iPad, iPadOS reporting Macintosh with touch) 홈 화면에 추가 opens #pwaWin with the three steps (공유 → 홈 화면에 추가 → open from the icon) and a line for other browsers. The button goes into the lobby's .tutil (before 전체화면; on a phone the ⋯ menu) whenever it can help (not already running as an app: display-mode standalone / fullscreen or navigator.standalone); on a touch device the lobby gets #pwaTip over the tab bar (추가하기 · ×, × kept in LS pwaTip; appinstalled sets it too). The update check: version.json (no-store) 8 s after boot, every 30 minutes while visible, and on coming back to the front (at most every 5 minutes); a build id other than the page's → #pwaNew (새 버전(v…)이 나왔어요 · 새로고침 · ×, × for this visit) on the menus, not in a match; back after 30 minutes or more with the title quiet (G.st menu, UI.open menu, not in a room, no field focused, no window open) → location.reload(), once per build (sessionStorage qzAuto). The tip and the banner sit under the windows (z-index 50) and go when a match starts (UI.hideAll / startMatch hooks).
- The 신규 소개 carousel (newsban.js NEW_CONTENT) opens with two feature cards: 앱으로 설치 (act pwa) and 빠른 매칭 (act qm), their icons the same SVGs at the card's 84 px (.nbi svg 1em). UI.act('pwa') works on every page, so the card's button always says something: the browser's prompt when kept; else #pwaWin for the case — iOS (공유 → 홈 화면에 추가), another browser (크롬 · 엣지: the address bar's install icon or the menu; 삼성 인터넷: 현재 페이지 추가 → 홈 화면), already an app, a page that cannot be one (the claude.ai artifact, a file from disk). QM.go without the account server opens the multiplayer screen instead of nothing. shop.js: a guest's sign-in question waits while #pwaWin is open, as it does for the carousel.
- Tests (1280×720 and 390×844, the page from 127.0.0.1): the worker in control with '/' kept (4.5 MB, this build's id inside); the manifest and its three icons (sizes as said, image/png), version.json's id = the page's; the head's links; an iPhone: the button 홈 화면에 추가, the how-to window, the tip (touch) and closing it for good; Android: a stand-in beforeinstallprompt kept (defaultPrevented), prompted, accepted → the button goes; running as an app → nothing offered; a newer build: the banner, gone in a match, back on the title; away 31 minutes → reloaded once (the navigation type reload, qzAuto set), the same build again → no second reload; then the HTTP server stopped and the page reloaded: it boots from the worker's copy (title, the lobby built, the worker in control).

## v6.80 — 빠른 매칭, quick match (quick.js new, built last; lobby.js notes; style.css; no server change)
- The owner's 3번 of the list after v6.78. The public rooms were listed (ROOMS, v6.12: qz_rooms) but a player had to read the list and pick one; QM picks and joins.
- QM.go (the lobby's 멀티플레이 card pill .mqm, or the multiplayer screen's gold button .qmgo above 방 만들기, which turns .mpmk): the multiplayer screen opens, the name is taken as 방 만들기 / 참가 take it, then up to MAX 3 tries: the list freshly loaded (fresh(): an in-flight ROOMS.load is waited for, then one more), the best room picked — ok(): status lobby (a playing room says ingame), a seat free, a 4-letter code not tried yet, a map this version has (MAPDEFS: else it is another version's room — the rows carry no version), a known mode, a chapter (scen + MAPDEFS[map].ep > 1) this player has opened (EP.unlocked); score = players × 10 + 25 for the player's mode (CFG.mpCfg.mode, else CFG.mode) + a little random — and NET.join'ed. watch(): in = NET.cli in that room's lobby; turned away = netFatal left the room (full, ingame, ver, gone); no answer in WAIT 15 s → NET.leave; either way the next try (NET.msg cleared). Nothing left (or three tries spent) → make(): CFG.mpPub = true, NET.create with the last room settings; signed in it is listed for the next quick matcher, a guest is told to sign in for that. The list not loading at all → its error is said, nothing made.
- The multiplayer screen on a phone: sideways (html[data-lob=l]) the left box wraps so 빠른 매칭 and 방 만들기 sit side by side and the code row stays in view (667×375 included); on short screens the hint goes and the two big buttons are lower.
- Stops: 취소 (the button during a search, qmstop: leaves a room being joined), 뒤로 / 방 만들기 / 참가 / a list row (UI.act wrapper). Notes over the waiting room (#migBox, done): 빠른 매칭 — 방에 들어왔어요 · 열린 방이 없어서 새 방을 만들었어요 (+ the line about the list). Not offered without the account server (ACC.on) or in claude.ai's own rooms (NET.kind room: no list there).
- Tests (stubbed list, joins and room making; 1280×720 and 390×844): the pill on the card; MUT5 (5 players, same mode) tried first, full → TDM2 joined, with a playing, a full, an unknown-map, a locked-chapter and a bad-mode room passed over; three rooms turning the player away (ver, ingame, full) → a public room made (CFG.mpPub false before); an empty list → a room at once; a room that never answers (WAIT 1.5 s) → the next; 취소 while joining; the list failing → the error shown, nothing made; leaving the screen; a guest's room (the sign-in line); NET.kind room → no pill, no button.

## v6.79 — the results on a phone (mobres.js new, built after mobroom.js; moblob.js calls MRES.relay; style.css)
- The owner's 1번 of the list after v6.78 (with 빠른 매칭 and the PWA to follow). Before: on a phone held sideways (844×390) the results were 644 px tall — the table started under the MVP card, and the buttons (sticky at the bottom since a sixteen-player table) sat over its first rows, so the scores needed a scroll; the HUD's text showed through the 78 % backdrop.
- mobres.js: after every UI.showResults (the outermost wrapper: the coins box, the PvP patch, the chapter's result, the hall of fame and the achievements are in by then), where html[data-lob] = 'l', #results.mres is laid out as a grid: .rsL (column 1: the title, the score line — a chapter's .epRes in the scenario —, the .hofRes / .coinRes / .misRes / .achRes boxes and the MVP, scrolling on its own) · .rsT (column 2: table.tbl, 대인전's .pvSide + two tables; the header row sticky, the player's tr.me scrolled to the middle when the table is longer than its box) · .mbtns (row 2 across both, no longer sticky). The boxes that arrive later find their places by class as before (fs.after, misResPut's box.after, the achievements' at.after are all inside .rsL); a box prepended to #results itself (a module that found no score line) is moved into .rsL under the score line by a MutationObserver (childList of #results, only while .mres). Turned upright or a bigger window (MOBL.apply → MRES.relay while the results show): flat() puts the parts back in their order, one column as before. Desktop untouched (no data-lob).
- style.css: under html[data-lob] the results' backdrop .95 (was .78: the HUD's round title and hints showed through); sideways the title 15 px, the score 21 px (the chapter result compact), the boxes full width with tighter padding, the MVP portrait 58 px, the table 12 px with nowrap cells and the name column cut at 130 px.
- lobby.js: the 공지 panel's short lines get v6.79's and v6.78's (v6.78 had left its line out).
- Tests (844×390 and 667×375): a guest (9 players: no page scroll, the table 6 px clear of the buttons); signed in with 16 players and the player last (the coins from the mock's claim, missions, achievements and a hall-of-fame line put in as the server would: the left column scrolls, the table scrolled to the player's row); a box prepended late lands in .rsL; upright (one column) and back (two); team deathmatch (two tables); chapter 1 (the chapter's result); 1280×720 one column as before.

## v6.78 — the phone rooms: the waiting room, 방 만들기, the multiplayer screen (mobroom.js new; moblob.js the typing guard; style.css)
- The owner's 1번 of the next-patch list after v6.77. Before: upright, the waiting room was about two screens tall (the settings and the map below the eight slots and the room chat; 방장 넘기기 squeezed the names); sideways the room chat sat off the screen; 방 만들기 ran past the screen.
- mobroom.js (built after moblob.js): after every UI.mpLobby / UI.buildSetup, where html[data-lob] is set, the window is laid out anew — lobby.js's markup is untouched and other modules still find .slots, .rside, .rchat, .sprev, .wchars by class: .dpHead · .mtabs · .mrbody (.mrpl the players: the .mrsum summary and .slots; .mrpan the panel: its parts tagged data-mt set / ch / chat) · .dpFoot; the window's data-mt picks the part shown (CSS). Waiting room upright: 플레이어 n/8 · 설정 · 캐릭터 · 채팅, one at a time; sideways the players on the left (slots in two columns) and 설정 · 캐릭터 · 채팅 as tabs on top of the panel. 방 만들기: 설정 · 캐릭터 (sideways the settings scroll on their own beside the map preview). The summary (mode · map or chapter, bots · difficulty, 설정 ›) opens 설정.
- The tab lives through every redraw (a player joining, a setting, the room chat); another room (NET.code) starts on its first tab with nothing unread; 방 만들기 opens on 설정. A room chat line (CHAT.add, not one's own) while 채팅 is not open puts a red dot on it. Turning the phone (MOBL.apply → MROOM.relay) draws the open window again for the new shape; a tab that shape does not have falls back to the first (플레이어 → 설정 sideways).
- Typing: in the room chat (html.rtype, focusin / focusout of #chatIn) the header, the footer and, sideways, the players step aside; 보내기 and 전체 keep the field focused (a capture-phase mousedown preventDefault: the chat bar stops mousedown from bubbling). MOBL.apply leaves the shape alone while any text field has the focus — a phone's keyboard makes the window short, and on a small phone held upright that could read as sideways; leaving a field applies the shape the window has by then (focusout → 200 ms).
- The waiting room's head in one row: upright the title and the 방 코드 words go (the code says enough), and under 400 px the code and its buttons (코드 복사 · 친구 초대 · 초대 링크) are smaller; upright the footer's connection line goes; sideways 방장 넘기기 takes two lines in a half-width slot and the ping stays inside the slot.
- The multiplayer screen: the whole screen, the open rooms' list taking what is left; upright one column (it first ran past the right edge: .mpwrap's align-items flex-start → stretch).
- Desktop (no data-lob) is untouched: MROOM.build returns at once and every new rule sits under html[data-lob].
- Tests: 390×844 and 360×640 upright, 844×390 and 667×375 sideways — the host: every tab, the summary, the chat dot and its clearing, a redraw keeping the room chat's focus and the tab; 방 만들기's two tabs; the MP screen; no page scroll, nothing past the right edge. Turning (a viewport step in the harness): 채팅 kept, 플레이어 → 설정 and back; typing and turning (nothing moves until the field is left; 방 만들기's title kept); 1280×720 the desktop window; a player who is not the host (7 controls disabled, 설정은 호스트가 정해요, 호스트가 시작하길 기다리는 중…, no 넘기기); a new room opening on 플레이어; 방 만들기 reopened on 설정. x12, x14, smoke, x2, x9, the tutorial, CSP, admin as before.

## v6.77 — the phone lobby (moblob.js new; lobby.js data-seg, footer .fst; gchat.js the chat tab's dot; style.css)
- The owner's word: "너는 ui ux 전문가야 모바일 환경에서 너무 화면이 좁고 답답하고 필요한 정보들을 넣을수가 없는 상태니까 너가 모바일 전용 로비 ui/ux를 만들어줘". Before: upright, the top bar took three rows and the four cards 456 px, leaving the three lower panels 12–68 px each; sideways, the lower panels were hidden altogether.
- moblob.js: MOBL.mode() → html[data-lob] where the desktop lobby does not fit (900 px wide or less, or 520 px tall or less): 'l' when wider than tall, else 'p'; '' otherwise (the desktop lobby, untouched); followed on resize / orientationchange (not while typing in the chat: a keyboard makes the window short). One markup, laid out anew by style.css — the modules' buttons stay where they are in the DOM (#menu .lobTop .tbtns etc.), so their handlers and dots keep working.
- p: the header one row (profile — the account button itself, so .aname goes —, coins, mail, ⋯ with settings · controls · patch notes · contact · fullscreen · landscape · language); 빠른 시작 as the hero (full width, 118–170 px, the characters on the right, ▶ title, the mode · map · difficulty tag) and 방 만들기 · 멀티플레이 · 사격장 as three tiles (84–118 px, title only, the 기초 훈련 pill kept); the lower panels as tabs (.lseg: 채팅 · 미션·전적 · 공지, from the panels' data-seg; the tab names the panel, so its title goes) filling the rest; .tbtns fixed along the bottom as a tab bar (상점 · 미션 · 업적 · 랭킹 · 인벤토리 · 친구, icon and word, 54 px, safe areas); the footer just the version and the legal links.
- l: a grid — the header row with the buttons as icons (업적 / 랭킹 stay tabs of the missions window there, as before), the hero tall on the left with the three tiles stacked beside it, the tabs on the right, the footer line.
- Tabs: the one picked is kept (LS lseg; 채팅 first by default, else the first there is); a tab out of view gets a red dot: 채팅 for unread lines (gchDot includes it; the chat counts as seen only while its tab is open — GCH.inPanel), 미션·전적 while the top bar's missions button has its dot (a MutationObserver on .tbtns). Typing in the lobby's chat (html.gtype, moblob.js mobType): the cards, the tab bar and the footer step aside so the chat takes the screen, until the field is left; 보내기's mousedown is cancelled so the field (and a phone's keyboard) stays.
- Tests: 390×844, 360×640, 430×932, 768×1024 upright; 844×390, 667×375 sideways; 1280×720 unchanged: guest and signed in, every tab, the ⋯ menu, the shop / missions / friends windows over the lobby, no page scroll, typing mode both ways (send keeps the focus, leaving restores), two pages upright (the chat tab's dot and clearing, the missions dot following the button's, a rebuild keeping the tab); x12, x14, smoke, x2, x9, the tutorial, CSP, admin as before.

## v6.76 — the chat in the lobby, room posts (gchat.js, lobby.js, style.css; no server change)
- The owner's words: 4번 of the next-patch list ("채팅으로 방 모집"), then, mid-way, "전체 채팅을 로비에 맵목록 지워버리고 거기에 박으면 될듯".
- The lobby: the lower right panel is the chat (lobby.js: `.lp.lchat` with 전체 채팅, the status in small.gcst and ⤢ = 크게 보기) where the map list was; the map list stays only where the chat cannot run (GCH.can() false). One box, #gchBox, moves between that panel and the floating window #gchWin (GCH.mount: the window when GCH.open, else the panel); what it shows lives through a move or a redraw (keep / back: the words typed, the cursor, the log's scroll — UI.buildTitle redraws the lobby on account changes). inPanel() = the title shows and the panel is laid out, 140 px tall or more, with the box in it (panelShown without the last). The top bar's 채팅 (.tgch) shows only at max-height 520 px (the lower panels are hidden) or max-width 900 px (one squeezed column): it opens the floating window. UI.show back to the title closes the floating window where the panel shows. Unread lines count only while the box is not on screen (dot on .tgch and on the waiting room's button). The name menu is #gcMenu on the page (fixed, by the name, above it near the bottom), closed by a click elsewhere or by scrolling the log; one delegated click handler serves [data-gc] in #gchBox, #gchWin, #gcMenu and .lchat. The online count is asked every minute while the box is on screen.
- Room posts: the host of a PeerJS waiting room (NET.on, host, kind peerjs, ui lobby, no hand-over) gets 「채팅에 모집」 in the waiting room's footer (others: 전체 채팅; an icon only under 620 px); it opens the floating window with the room in the composer (모집 chip, × removes it). The line carries r = {c code, m mode (NET_MODES), p map, n players, x seats (NET_MAXP)}, signed with the text (data: …\n#room:c|m|p|n|x); no words → 같이 하실 분!. Refused: not the host any more, a full room (mpFull), a second post within 30 s. Receivers drop a malformed r (code ^[A-Z0-9]{4}$, a known mode, map ^[a-z0-9_]{1,24}$, 1 ≤ n ≤ x ≤ 8) and a sender's second room post within 20 s; the card shows mode · map (a chapter: 시나리오: name) · n/x명 · 방 CODE and 참가 (내 방 on one's own; after 10 minutes 지난 모집, greyed). 참가 → INVT.go(code) as an invite (the multiplayer screen, NET.join); inside a room it says to leave first (or that one is in it). Over the waiting room the floating window sits at the top (inroom) so 시작 / 나가기 stay in reach. Old v6.75 pages drop room posts (their signature check does not cover r).
- Tests: x14 (two pages, a stubbed PeerJS host): the footer buttons for host and player, the composer chip, a post with no words, the 30 s refusal, the card and 참가 (the tutorial's question shows for a new account, the join goes on), 참가 from inside a room, a signature without r, a bad code / seats / mode, a post too soon, one after 20 s (a chapter card), a card turning 지난 모집, a player cannot post, the dot on the waiting room's button, a full room. x12 again on the panel (all of v6.75's cases). Screenshots: desktop lobby with the panel, the name menu over it, a guest's panel, phone upright (the top bar's 채팅 → the sheet) and sideways (no panels → the button), the waiting room on desktop and phone.

## v6.75 — global chat (gchat.js new; schema.sql chat_keys, qz_chat_key / qz_chat_who / qz_online, the channel's two policies, gacha_config.chat_on, qz_ping chat; admin.js the switch; build.py CSP; style.css)
- The owner's word ("현재 접속자들끼리 대화를 나눌 수 있는 전체채팅방 만들어줘 이건 채팅내용 저장 ㄴㄴ 바로바로 날려줘"): a lobby chat for the players online, nothing stored, every line sent on at once.
- Transport: Supabase Realtime Broadcast over one WebSocket (Phoenix vsn 1.0.0, no supabase-js): wss://<ref>.supabase.co/realtime/v1/websocket?apikey=KEY&vsn=1.0.0, topic realtime:qz-lobby joined with config {broadcast {ack false, self false}, presence off, private true} and the player's access token; heartbeat every 25 s (one left unanswered by the next → a new socket), access_token pushed whenever ACC.setSes runs (and a token under 5 minutes from expiry is refreshed every minute). A private join refused for lack of policies (Unauthorized …) → the public channel of the same name (public and private channels do not hear each other). Reconnects after 1, 2, 5, 10, 20, 30 s. Client broadcasts are never written to realtime.messages; Realtime only runs the policies at a join. build.py: the index's connect-src gains exactly wss://<ref>.supabase.co/realtime/v1/websocket (admin / legal pages do not).
- When the socket is open: signed in, G.st menu, the chat not switched off, the server has the chat, and the tab in view (or hidden under 2 minutes). A match (startMatch) closes it and the window; back in the lobby it joins again. The window (#gchWin, the lobby's 「채팅」 button .tgch after 친구, a red dot for unread lines while closed) keeps the last 80 lines in memory only.
- Identity: each page makes an ECDSA P-256 pair (WebCrypto, private half not extractable), qz_chat_key(p_pub raw point base64url) → {kid (20 hex of sha-256(pub:uid)), nickname, ms}; an account keeps its 3 newest keys, one new key every 5 s (too_soon → one retry after 5.2 s). A line = {k kid, ts, t text, s signature over kid\nts\ntext}. A receiver asks qz_chat_who(kids, 20 a call, batched 150 ms; kept 2 min, an unknown key 1 min) → {pub, n, tt, ft, b suspended, me} and shows a line only if: the signature checks, the key is someone's and not suspended, not a player I blocked (ACC.blk, by nickname), ts within 10 minutes of the server's clock and newer than that key's last, not seen before, and the key's 7th within 15 s is dropped; 40 checks a second at most. Sending: 120 characters (control characters out), one every 1.5 s, 5 in 15 s (the page's steady clock, performance.now). ts is by the server's clock (GCH.off from qz_chat_key / qz_online ms, half the round trip), so a computer with a wrong clock still gets through.
- Schema: chat_keys (kid, user_id cascade, pub, at; RLS on, no policies, revoked); qz_chat_ok() (signed in, chat_on, not suspended); qz_chat_key (qz_guard, chat_off, bad_key, too_soon); qz_chat_who (SQL, authenticated only; x = in my blocks); qz_find_player takes k:<kid>; qz_online() → {n: last_seen within 150 s, ms, on}; qz_ping adds chat (chat_on). The DO block makes "qz lobby chat read" (select to authenticated, extension broadcast, topic qz-lobby) and "qz lobby chat send" (insert, the same + qz_chat_ok()) on realtime.messages, or raises a notice where there is no realtime schema.
- The switch: gacha_config.chat_on (admin 게임 설정 → 「해독기 · 초대 · 채팅」 → 전체 채팅). Off: no new keys, the send policy refuses, qz_ping (every 25 s) / qz_online / qz_chat_key tell the pages, which close the socket and say 「지금은 전체 채팅이 꺼져 있어요」. A database without qz_chat_key / qz_chat_who / qz_online (PGRST202) → GCH.old: 「서버가 업데이트되면 채팅을 쓸 수 있어요」, no socket.
- The name menu: 친구 요청 and 차단 go by the line's key — 'k:' + kid, which qz_find_player resolves through chat_keys (22 characters, never a nickname; a nickname reading like someone's friend code would otherwise find the code's owner) — 차단 hides that player's lines at once (who entries marked x); qz_chat_who's x (I blocked them) hides them after a nickname change too, and an unblock (ACC.unblock) asks who is who again. 신고: the inquiry form, report, with that player's nickname, last line and its time filled in — the only way a line is ever kept.
- Costs (Supabase): a broadcast counts 1 + one per listener (every signed-in player in the lobby is a listener). Free: 200 connections, 100 messages/s (over it Realtime drops connections; the page reconnects), 2 million a month; Pro 500 / 500 / 5 million, then $2.50 a million.
- Tests: local Postgres 16 — fresh, upgrade from v6.74, a re-run, a database without realtime (the notice); t75.sql: keys (too_soon, bad_key, three kept, ms), who, a suspended account (no key; its send refused by the policy), qz_online, block / unblock by k:<kid> (who's x on and off, an unknown key not_found, a nickname set to another player's friend code finds that player by name but the right one by key), the policies on a stand-in realtime.messages (a player's insert passes, a suspended one's and another topic's are refused), anon refused, the switch (chat_off, the send refused, qz_online on / qz_ping chat false, on again). Two pages of one browser (x12, a Phoenix stand-in over a BroadcastChannel): both join privately; a line both ways (escaped, the unread dot, one who call); a forged signature, an unknown key, a non-string signature, a replay, an older line and one 11 minutes ahead dropped; nine lines straight onto the socket → six shown; the sender's limit; 차단 from the name menu hides that player's lines then and later; a suspended sender's line dropped, shown again once lifted; a clock 20 minutes fast: dropped without the server's clock, through with it; a match closes the socket, the lobby joins again; a refreshed session pushes access_token; the private join refused → public; a silent link → a new socket; the switch off and on; a server without the chat; signing out clears it. The CSP lets exactly the realtime socket path through on index.html; the regression batch (smoke, x2, x3, x6a, x6b, x8, x9, the tutorial, CSP, admin) as before. Desktop and phone screenshots, the admin switch.

## v6.74 — security patch, part 3 (schema.sql match tickets, caps, nicknames, blocks, deleted identities, hall-of-fame tools; account.js / shop.js tickets; friends.js blocks; invite.js texts; net.js movement; admin.js; privacy.html)
- The owner's 3차 ("1,2,3,4,5,6,7 ㄱㄱ", after the audit list: 1 scenario first clears / the hall of fame forged, 2 achievement counters with no time limit, 3 the ranking counting idle time and its day limits above a person's, 4 a ban dodged by deleting and signing up again, 5 nicknames shared (titles copied), 6 an invite link making friends without the other's yes, 7 positions never checked). The crate-gun ownership check (P2P) stays put off at his word.
- Match tickets (schema.sql): match_tokens (RLS, no policies) — qz_match_start(p_mode orig|mut|tdm|ffa|bomb|race|scen, p_ep, p_diff, p_match) → {token}: one every 2 s per account (too_soon), a new one ends the account's unused ones, used ones go after a day; qz_guard refuses a banned account. qz_claim and qz_round_claim take p_token (old signatures dropped). A good ticket for qz_claim: the account's, unused (it is used up by the claim), under 3 hours old, the same mode (the scenario: the same chapter and difficulty). The minutes a claim counts (v_w) = since the ticket (never more than since the last paid match; 45, 60 in the scenario); without a good ticket 10 at most. coin_log detail tok / w. Rounds: a ticket of the mode not used or used in the last 2 minutes bounds the round's minutes by the match's; without one 3 at most.
- The scenario: a chapter's clear needs the good ticket, a clear time no longer than the ticket's time + 30 s (a sped-up game fails it too) and at least qz_ep_min(ep, diff) — [200, 200, 200, 270], [150, 150, 150, 160], [200, 200, 200, 210], [170, 170, 170, 270], [260, 260, 260, 350] seconds (easy … expert): about 0.7 of the fastest clear of four bots that could not be hurt and hit three times as hard (accelerated runs of every chapter: 196–359 s on easy / normal, 233–509 s on expert; normal bots that could be hurt took 470–550 s). The 4-minute floor of v6.21 is gone (bots cleared chapter 2 in 3:16). The hall of fame takes the ticket's match id (no ticket: none — the clear does not count anyway); a member's ticket started before the row was made (+1 min); the ep_firsts row is locked while the team is counted (no ninth member in a race). The scenario's kill cap a match is 400 (80 elsewhere) at 16 a minute.
- Day limits: gacha_config.rank_day_kills / rank_day_inf / rank_day_xp (2,000 / 400 / 100,000 by default; 2,000 / 800 / 150,000 were fixed before; checks 0 … 100,000 / 10,000,000; the admin page's 게임 설정 → 「랭킹 하루 상한」). Kills stay at 2,000 because the scenario's horde gives a strong team 8–12 a minute (bots 6–12). Achievement counters (qz_ach_day): hs 1,000, dmg 2,000,000, wins 40, mvp 40, pk 1,500, pw 40, rw 15, kn 300, cu 100, mk 1,000, cr 40, bm 80, surv 200, zw 200 a Korean day (profiles.cap_ach, reset with cap_day; qz_ach_left cuts each addition to what is left), and a match's own by its minutes: damage 20,000 a minute (60,000 a match), cures 2, marks 15, crates 1 + ½, bombs 1 + 1, melee kills ≤ kills; a win / MVP (wins, mvp, pw, rw) only from a match of 3 minutes or more. Missions take the same damage. p_ach over 400 characters is ignored.
- Nicknames (schema.sql): a unique index on lower(nickname) (profiles_nick_lower), made after the upgrade renames the duplicates — the oldest account keeps a shared name, the others become their first 9 characters + _ + friend code (a second pass with the id's letters if that meets yet another). qz_set_nickname: nick_short (under 2), nick_taken (another player's, any case; a race on the index answers the same); qz_admin_rename: nick_taken. qz_auto_nick: 4 digits × 8 tries, then 6, then 8 digits. qz_new_profile(uid, nick, p_welcome) (the old two-argument one dropped): a taken nickname at sign-up becomes 생존자 + digits, a unique violation at that very moment is retried (4 times). qz_nick_free(p_nick) (anon too) → {ok, nick, why}: the sign-up form asks first (account.js ACC.nickFree; an older database: not asked). The game asks for a nickname for 생존자 + 4 to 8 digits now (shop.js nickAsk).
- Blocks (schema.sql): blocks (a blocked b; RLS, no policies); qz_block(p_who) (500 at most, block_full; the friendship, requests either way and invites between the two go; banned accounts may block) → the friend list; qz_blocks() → {blocked:[{code, nickname, at}]}; qz_unblock(p_who) → the same; qz_friends gains blocked (my count). qz_friend_add: you_blocked (mine) / friend_blocked (theirs); qz_dm_send (dm_not_friend), qz_invite (inv_friends), qz_gift (not_friends) refuse a blocked pair too. friends.js: 차단 on a request and behind 삭제 on a friend → a confirm in the row (blkAsk / blkNote) → qz_block; 「차단 목록」 heading with 보기 / 접기 (frtg) and 해제 per player.
- Invite links: qz_ref_set sends a friend request from the new account to the inviter (unless a pair or a block is there, or the inviter's list is full) instead of a friendship; invite.js's texts say so. An account marked reborn cannot take a link (ref_reborn).
- Deleted identities (schema.sql): qz_secret (one random pepper, made once), deleted_ids (h = sha-256 of pepper : 'e:' lower-case e-mail | 'p:' provider ':' account id; keep_until = 30 days after the deletion or the end of a suspension it was under, whichever is later; banned_until / ban_reason). qz_idhs(email, provider, id) makes the hashes. qz_account_delete keeps the account's e-mail and each auth.identities row's e-mail and provider id (if auth.identities cannot be read, the e-mail only) and drops expired rows. qz_on_signup (never failing on this part) hashes the new e-mail and, for Google / Kakao, raw_user_meta_data provider_id / sub: a match still kept → profiles.reborn, no welcome coins (qz_new_profile p_welcome false), tut_done set (no 기초 훈련 reward), no invite link, no to-all mail meant for new accounts (qz_mail_open); a suspension still running is carried over (banned_until, ban_reason) and noted in admin_log as ban_carry (no admin). privacy.html: a row in 3 (탈퇴한 계정의 식별값 — 30일, 정지 중이면 정지가 끝날 때까지, 영구 정지는 계속) and the note in 7; legal.json effective 2026-10-18.
- Hall of fame tools: qz_admin_suspects('hof') → each chapter × difficulty's row (time, the floor min, src match / past / refill, the team with ids, codes, banned); qz_admin_hof_del(p_ep, p_diff, p_refill, p_reason) (owner): the row and its team go; refill = the earliest first clear left on ep_records (not the old team, not banned) and those within 3 minutes of it, 8 at most, no time; admin_log hof_del with the old team's ids. admin.js: 의심 유저 → 「명예의 전당」 (빠름 tag under 1.15 × min, 지우기 with 다음 팀으로 채우기 / 비워 두기), log labels hof_del / ban_carry, errors nick_taken / no_record.
- The game (account.js / shop.js): shopTicket — once startMatch's wrappers are done (a microtask, so G.mt is set), a signed-in match of a paying mode asks ACC.matchStart({mode, ep: cfg.ep, diff, mt: G.mt}) (retried twice after 2.5 s on too_soon / network); G.mtok rides on shopMatchStats / shopRoundStats as tok → p_token. A database before v6.74: qz_match_start missing → noTicket (asked no more); p_token refused → the claim drops it first, the round claim once (oldRound). Errors: nick_taken, nick_short, you_blocked, friend_blocked, block_full, ref_reborn.
- Movement (net.js, host): ngMove on every state of a living remote player before netSample — a budget filling at 9 m/s across the ground up to 16 m and 6 m/s upward up to 6 m; a state past it is taken only as far as the budget goes (the others see that player catch up at 9 m/s; its claims are checked from there), NG.rej mv / mvu; falling is never held back. ngShove credits the shoves the host sends (netPost kx / kz / up; SCEN.pushHuman), up to 40 m over the budget. placeAt on the host (a respawn, a round, a zone) → ngPlaced: the budget starts there, and for 3 s a state from far away (the old spot) is held at the new place. A body put back from under the map (game.js: 0, 2, 5) is taken when the last state was under −12. Nothing is kicked. Calibration: accelerated bot matches of every mode and chapter (positions over 0.1–4 s windows): about 7.5 m/s held at most (bunny hops; a berserk rager 8.2 by the numbers), 8 m in 0.5 s with a knock-back, 5.5 m up in 4 s.
- Tests: local Postgres 16 — fresh, upgrade from v6.73 (duplicate nicknames renamed: Dup / dup → dup_<code>, a 16-character name → 9 + _ + code; the index made) and a re-run; t74.sql: sign-ups (a taken nickname → 생존자 + 4 digits), nick_taken / nick_short / qz_nick_free (anon too), the index holding; tickets (too_soon, bad_mode, a 20-minute ticket: kills 80, infections 30, score 3,300, dmg 60,000, mk 200, cu 20, cr 10, bm 21, kn 40, mvp / win; the same ticket again → tok false, w 10, mk +150; another mode's → false; a 2-minute match → no MVP / win, w 2); the scenario (two pages of one match clear and share the hall of fame, 150 s / longer than its ticket / no ticket → no clear; the first rewards twice); the day limit (rank_day_kills 150 → day_kills [80, 0]; a negative value refused); rounds (score cut to 1,600 with a 5-minute ticket, 400 without; surv stopping at 200); blocks (the friendship gone, you_blocked / friend_blocked / inv_friends / dm_not_friend, the list, unblocking, a new request); an invite link → a request, not a friendship; deleting (a suspended account: 3 hashes kept with the ban, a plain one: 1; all 64-hex) and coming back by the e-mail in other case and by the Google account → reborn, 0 coins, the suspension and its reason, tut set, admin_log ban_carry; someone new → 3,000; ref_reborn, the tutorial 0, the new-player mail hidden from the reborn only; past keep_until → a normal account; the admin hall of fame view, hof_del with refill (2 members), no_record, rename nick_taken. Headless (the mock: qz_match_start, qz_nick_free, blocks, nickname rules, referral requests, p_token recorded / refused on __MOCK_NOTOKEN): x9 — sign-up refused for ALPHA after Alpha, setNick taken / short texts, the ticket asked once with mode, difficulty and G.mt, tok on both stats and in the claim body, an old database's p_token dropped (claim and round), no ticket on __MOCK_NOTICKET or the range, blocking from the friends window (the row's confirm, 0 friends, the list, unblocking), the invite popup's request text and the inviter's incoming request, nickAsk for 6 digits. Movement: x11 (a fake link through netOnState: walking 7 m/s free, a 50 m teleport → 16 m then 9 m/s, a speed hack held, +20 m up → 6, falling free, a shove credited, a respawn's late state held, a dead player unchecked) and x10 (every player-like actor of accelerated bot matches through ngMove at 30 Hz: mut, orig, tdm, ffa, bomb, race, every chapter — about 2 million states, no state held back; also with no shove credit at all, after the under-the-map rule). Regressions: smoke, markup (x2), host checks (x3), snapshot rows (x8), deletion (x6a / x6b), the tutorial, CSP (pages and sockets), the admin page.

## v6.73 — blood & gore locked at 15세 (util.js GORE, ui.js CFG, lobby.js options; no server change)
- The owner's 2번 (with the weapon names, which wait for his OK on a name table: the first table was lost from the working record, a new one goes to him first). For the public release 15세 is the most: GORE.init / GORE.set read anything but 0 as 1, CFG.gore is put to 1 unless it is 0, and the options' 피 표현 offers 15세 (순화) and 피 끄기 only. The lv-2 drawing (dismemberment, bursting heads, pools, torn skins) stays in the code, unreachable.
- Tests: a saved 원본 (qz_cfg gore 2) loads as 15세 (GORE.lv 1, CFG.gore 1), the options show the two choices, GORE.set(2) gives 1.

## v6.72 — security patch, part 2 (net.js host checks, flood limits, host migration; account.js / shop.js re-sign-in to delete; schema.sql qz_account_delete, referrals)
- The owner's 2차 ("1번 다하고 2번 ㄱㄱ": this, then the weapon names). From the audit of v6.70: hit claims trusted in the zombie modes (C1), NaN in claims and effects (C2), no per-link message limit (H1), the host migration that one page could steer (H2), claws and effects with no range (M1, M2); deleting an account with a token alone (DB H4) and the invite cap undone by deleting the invited accounts (DB H3).
- The host's checks (net.js, before HOSTH): ngTrack (NET.frameEnd, host, in a match) keeps every actor's last 0.8 s of positions (every 1/30 s, a ring of 24 with the eye height) and, for a remote player, when each weapon was last in its inventory or reported in hand (ngW) and when it was last a living human (ngHum); netOnState notes a grenade seen thrown (ngThr). ngClaim(src, t, h): the weapon index an integer naming a WPN entry; the weapon held within 8 s (16 s for what flies or lingers), a grenade's blast only within 8 s of a throw; the shooter a living human within 1 s (8 s for a blast / splash / thrown); 대인전: foe + PVP.claimOk as before; the zombie modes: a zombie target, damage ≤ ngProf(w).cap (the weapon's biggest damage-like number anywhere in its entry — dmg, spDmg, cmbDmg, swDmg, bayDmg, hsDmg, gDmg, nested gMode… — at least 60, × max(head multiplier, 3) × 2.2; a blast ≥ 1,000), and one time budget a player (ngSpend: a claim costs 1 / per s, per = shots a second (the biggest rpm-like number; a blade 1 / its fastest swing) × pellets / spFan × pen / pierce / fan / slugs (× its widest cleave) × 2, 24 targets a shot for splash weapons — proj, cone, flame, air, cluster, slam, spFlame, special, grenades — a blast at least 48 a second; 1.5 s held at a time); every mode: the target within reach of the shooter's recent positions (bullet 110 m + 6, W.range for the railgun, a blade's longest reach + 3, 170 m for splash / blasts) and, for a plain bullet (pistol / smg / rifle / mg / shotgun / sniper with no pen, pierce, rail, wallK, trans or alt) unless the target is very big (epRay / xRay / boss / ZBIG / over 0.9 m wide or 3.2 m tall), a clear line (losClear) from one of the shooter's eye positions to the hit point — when that point is by one of the target's positions — or to its chest or head. The numbers taken are bounded: direction ±1, knockback ≤ 2 × the weapon's biggest kb-like number + 2 (16 for a blast), lift ≤ its biggest up-like number + 3 (8 for a blast), stagger ≤ 3, the hit point finite. NG.rej counts the reasons. Claims go 48 a message from a client (the host takes 64).
- Claws: a living zombie past its first-strike lock, 4 a second, within CLAW_R heavy + the target's half width + 2.2 m of the human in the last 0.8 s, in sight. Effects (eff): within 120 m; a shove (kx, kz ≤ 40 m/s) or a lift (≤ 22) on anyone (a zombie bomb launches zombies), statuses only on the other side (freeze ≤ 5 s, root / stagger / dizzy / shriek ≤ 4 s, grab / hold ≤ 2 s, burn ≤ 6 s with a real weapon, fields other files add such as THANATOS-9's slow ≤ 10 s). pong's timestamp must be a number. A client's state with anything but numbers in its 13 slots is dropped whole (positions within 2 km, speeds 60 m/s); at most 48 effects ride one state, each ≤ 24 entries of numbers / short words. A client skips a snapshot row with anything but numbers in its first ten.
- Links (netRx): on the host a message over 64 KiB is dropped unread (counts as 200), about 300 events a second (600 at once) and 90 states a second get through; the dropped count fades over about 4 s and past 1,200 the link is closed with bye 'flood' (T mpFlood on that page; its player goes on as a bot). A client takes 512 KiB a message from its host; the queue holds 5,000.
- Host migration (H2): the pages an asker found gone count only when it asks from further down the line than this page (so its word can make this page or one ahead of it the host, never itself); migto is followed only to a page ahead of the one that sent it, else this page marks that one passed and goes on in its own order. NET_VER r11 (everyone in a room on this version).
- Not changed (accepted for P2P, said to the owner): positions are not checked (speed / teleport), every page receives everything (wallhack / ESP), a remote player's crate / EH guns are trusted (loadout.js crateOK), the claude.ai room transport's self-chosen keys.
- Account deletion (schema.sql qz_account_delete): only a session signed into in the last 10 minutes — auth.sessions.created_at of the JWT's session_id; where that cannot be read, auth.users.last_sign_in_at — else 'reauth'. The game: an e-mail account's password step is a new sign-in already; a Google / Kakao account (no password) gets 「Google로 다시 로그인」 when this browser's last fresh sign-in (qz_signAt: password, OAuth return, e-mail link, sign-up) is over 9.5 minutes old; ACC.oauth(p, 'del') notes qz_after and, back from the provider, the delete window opens by itself (ACC.takeAfter, 10 minutes). The mock keeps each session's sign-in time (born, kept on refresh; window.__MOCK_SES_AGE ages them).
- Invites (schema.sql): profiles.ref_paid counts the invite rewards an account has been paid, for good (filled from the rewarded rows on the upgrade); qz_ref_check counts the cap as the larger of it and the rewarded rows and adds one when it pays; a suspended inviter is not paid; qz_ref_info / qz_me show the same count. Run schema.sql.
- Tests: the host handlers driven straight (a bot made a remote player on a fake link): a rifle body hit and head hit taken, 5,000 refused (dmg), a gun never held refused (held), bad weapon indexes (weapon), a grenade blast before / after a throw seen (throw / taken), junk numbers bounded (the hit stands), 60 pistol claims at once → 20 taken (rate), a target behind a wall refused even with a hit point said to be in the open (los), 300 m away (far), a human target in a zombie mode (team); a claw from 40 m refused, from 2 m taken; effects: a freeze from a zombie's page on a zombie dropped, a shove bounded to 40 m/s, a dizzy on a human bounded to 4 s; a state with a word in it dropped, a good one sampled; junk effects riding a state dropped; 700 events → 600, a flood kept up → the link closed and its player a bot; an 80 KiB message dropped. Every human bot's real hits sent through the checks as claims in a real match (Mutation 428 claims, TDM 66, the chapter 3 scenario 40): no damage, rate, reach, held or sight refusals — only a few 'dead' (shots in the frame a bot came back, fixed: a living human now counts) and 대인전's own claimOk. A client taking snapshot rows (hp, armour, the stats block; a row with a word skipped). Deleting: an e-mail account (password → deleted), a Google account with an hour-old sign-in (the window shows 「Google로 다시 로그인」, the server answers reauth), back from Google (the window opens by itself, deleted). Regression: the v6.71 markup / CSP checks, the tutorial all the way, Mutation / TDM / 폭파미션 / scenario starts, no CSP violations. Local Postgres 16: fresh, upgrade from v6.71 (rewarded invites counted into ref_paid) and a re-run; an old session / no session id refused, a fresh one deletes; auth.sessions gone → last_sign_in_at (old refused, fresh deletes); cap 2: two paid, the third not, a paid one deleted, the next still not; a suspended inviter not paid. Multiplayer between real pages not tested here (the owner tests it).

## v6.71 — security patch, part 1 (net.js, ui.js, lobby.js, pvp.js, bomb.js, scenario.js, episode.js, shop.js; build.py CSP; vercel.json headers; admin.js; schema.sql)
- From the owner's audit request (보안 · 치팅 점검), the first of three parts he okayed ("1차 ㄱㄱ"): markup from another player, the New Year letters with no daily limit, unbounded text in the coin log, the owner's address in these notes. Part 2 (the host checking what clients claim in every mode, account deletion and the referral count, message rate limits) and part 3 (server match tokens and the rest) wait.
- What another page sends (net.js): netRx runs netScrub on every message before anything reads it — the characters < > " ' and the backtick taken out of every string at any depth, a chat line's text (t 'chat', m) the one exception (always shown escaped), past 8 levels dropped. netHandle dispatches only own properties of HOSTH / CLIH. The client handlers make the host's values the type and size they should be: netCleanLob / netCleanCfg (keys `[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]{0,23}`, 48 at most; numbers finite, strings `[A-Za-z0-9_.:-]` ≤ 24; mode one of NET_MODES, map a MAPDEFS key), netCleanPl (name ≤ 16, skin from HSKINS, zombie class from ZLIST, ping −1..99999), netCleanRo (≤ 96 rows of ≤ 9), netCleanPlan (round number, placements as numbers); hosts / infect / rv zombie classes via netZ; end's score as two integers; netOnSnap's g, hp / armour / level / money and the stats block (kills, infections, deaths as integers, ping −1..99999). Scenario / chapter values the host sends (lives, wave, best, end record, an upgrade's weapon and level) are numbers / checked keys. A relay reason (room kind) must be a short lowercase word; relayWhyT shows only its known texts.
- Where they are shown (defence in depth): esc() also escapes ' and the backtick; the round result card, results, scoreboard, the 대인전 / 폭파미션 boards and the level HUD print numbers through |0; the map thumbnail's data-m escaped; the waiting room's ping |0. The unused waiting room in net.js (UI.mpLobby / UI.mpRender, overridden by lobby.js) is gone.
- CSP (build.py with_csp): the standalone index, admin.html, privacy.html and terms.html get a `<meta http-equiv="Content-Security-Policy">`: script-src only the SHA-256 of each inline script (index 3: three, peerjs, the game; admin 1; legal 1 — the build stops if the count is off), style 'self' 'unsafe-inline', img / media 'self' data: blob:, font 'self' data:, connect-src 'self' + the Supabase URL read from sb.js (+ https / wss 0.peerjs.com on the game page), worker-src blob: (net.js's timer worker), object-src 'none', base-uri 'none', form-action 'self'. Inline event handlers can never run, whatever markup gets in. The claude.ai artifact (dist/index.html) gets none. vercel.json: frame-ancestors 'none' + X-Frame-Options DENY (no framing — to embed the game somewhere, that site's origin goes in frame-ancestors), nosniff, strict-origin-when-cross-origin, Permissions-Policy off for camera / mic / location / payment / usb.
- Admin page: the sign-in (qz_adm_ses) lives in sessionStorage — this tab only, gone when it closes; an older copy in localStorage moves over once and is wiped. 게임 설정 → 해독기: 「하루 근·하·신·년 글자 상한」 (ny_day_cap).
- Letters (schema.sql): qz_ny_add keeps at most gacha_config.ny_day_cap letters (default 60) a Korean day on top of the per-match quota (profiles.ny_day / ny_day_n); answers day_left and day_cap. The game (shop.js) toasts 「오늘 받을 수 있는 글자를 다 받았어요 (하루 N개)」 when the day's cap is what kept some back (an older server without day_left: no toast). The mock models it (window.__MOCK_NYCAP).
- Text into the database: qz_claim / qz_round_claim keep the coin_log mode as `[a-z]` ≤ 12; qz_invite keeps map `[A-Za-z0-9_-]` ≤ 24 and mode `[a-z]` ≤ 12; qz_err_report (anyone may call it), qz_inquiry_send, qz_dm_send and qz_claim's match id cut their text to a few times the kept length before any regex work (over-long messages are still refused as before). Run schema.sql.
- NOTES.md no longer carries the owner's private address.
- The local harness (play.py, not committed) polls __ready with evaluate: wait_for_function evaluates a string in the page, which the CSP refuses.
- Tests: two layers of the multiplayer markup (straight into CLIH: lobby map, ping, a bad skin / class; the stats block through netOnSnap; a scenario 'scf' through netRx) → nothing runs, values come out typed; netScrub on deep / chat messages; markup injected straight into the page → blocked by the CSP (script-src-attr); no CSP violation on boot, through the full tutorial, Mutation / TDM / 폭파미션 / scenario starts and on the admin, privacy and terms pages; fetch and WebSocket reach only the hosts each page allows (DevTools network block to tell CSP from network); the admin session move, same-tab reload, new tab → sign-in, the cap field; the letters toast (cap hit, cap zero, normal, older server). Local Postgres 16: fresh, upgrade from v6.70 and a re-run; 8-letter matches → 60 then 0, the next day again, the owner sets 10 (8 + 2), a negative refused, staff refused; modes, invite map / mode, error report, DM and inquiry lengths; anon-callable functions unchanged. Multiplayer between real pages not tested here (the owner tests it).

## v6.70 — 기초 훈련, the tutorial (tutorial.js, built last; range.js keys; lobby.js range card button; newsban.js feature buttons; account.js me.tut; schema.sql qz_tut_claim)
- The owner picked it from the launch list (2 of 1, 2: the weapon renames wait for his OK on the name plan). A first-time player had nothing that showed the controls or the rules.
- It is the shooting range with TUT.on: Main.startTut (hides the lobby and the news banner, TUT.pending through Main.startRange) → the RANGE.begin hook runs TUT.setup: the range's record panel, its K / J keys (range.js) and moving targets are off, chat is off (CHAT.where null: Enter belongs to the tutorial), the top bar says 기초 훈련, the player gets AK-47 / USP / knife. #hTut (in #app, z 8 over the touch layer, pointer-events only on its button) walks through TUT_STEPS, each done only when it is really done: look (|Δyaw| + |Δpitch|/2 ≥ 2.4 rad), move (6 m on the ground), jump + crouch, 10 hits on the range dummies, 3 headshots, a reload (the magazine cut to 3 on entering; started and finished), a pick from the buy menu (Main.buyItem hook: the hand, the bag or the armour changed; the menu closes itself), the knife (a brute training dummy put behind the line at z 3.4, 3,000 hp, standing; out + a knife hit), a grenade (2 HE given; thrown, 1.4 s), three training zombies (rager, 330 hp, at 36–46 m walking up at .5–.62 of their speed to the player's x in front of the counters; they never strike), the rules (T('rules') + the human skills + zombie keys; Enter / 계속). A step done: 「✓ 좋아요!」 for .9 s. Enter (a button too, on a phone the only way) skips after 10 s; skipped steps show – at the end. Keyboard and phone wording differ (TOUCH.on: the stick, drag to look, the ↑ ↓ ↻ buttons, 구매, the weapon bar, auto-fire when it is on). Markers (.tutMk, not .off — that class hides anything) over the dummy and the zombies with the distance; behind the camera they sit at the bottom edge (「↓ 뒤에 있어요」). The panel hides under the pause menu and the buy menu.
- The end: LS qz_tutDone; #tutEnd holds the game as Main.overlay 'tut' (letting the mouse go does not pause; the game's keys wait; Enter / Esc → the lobby): the steps, the reward line, 「사격장에서 계속 연습」 (the plain range again: the tutorial's actors removed, the panel back, movers on) or 「로비로」. Leaving half-way (pause → 나가기) cleans up through RANGE.end.
- Reward: qz_tut_claim (schema.sql; profiles.tut_done; 1,000 coins once per account, coin_log 'tutorial', admin page 「기초 훈련 보상」; banned accounts get qz_guard's error; anon cannot). qz_me tut → ACC.me.tut (undefined on an older database: nothing is asked). Signed in: claimed at the end (+1,000 / already had it / failed → tried again on the next load). A guest is told to sign in; on the next sign-in an ACC.sub hook claims it once (toast). Run schema.sql.
- Lobby: the range card's 「▶ 기초 훈련」 (gold, pulsing, +1,000 badge while it is to be had; gone after it is done). A player who has never played (recGet().g 0), has not done it and was not asked on this browser gets #tutAsk once on 빠른 시작 / 방 만들기 / 멀티플레이 (기초 훈련 시작 / 바로 할게요 — the latter goes on with what was clicked). The news banner's first card is the tutorial with its own button (newsban.js: e.act / e.btn → UI.act).
- Tests: deterministic stepping (gameUpdate with the loop stopped) through all eleven steps → the end → more practice → the lobby (button plain); a touch run 844×390 (skip and continue tapped through the touch layer, the end window, the lobby); English texts; a signed-in end (the reward +1,000, me.tut); the real loop with real keys (W, Space, C, R, B → 2 → 1, 3, 4, Deadshot 6) for the input paths; leaving from the pause menu (actors, markers, panel gone), the plain range afterwards; the question once (not after playing, not after finishing); a guest's reward on sign-in once; an older database. Local Postgres 16: fresh, upgrade from v6.69 and a re-run; the claim twice (1,000 then 0), banned, anon refused. No page errors.

## v6.69 — M4A1 다크나이트-2, a crate gun built to the owner's design sheet (m4dk2.js after m4dk; schema.sql gun_prices row; m4dk.js dkOwnN)
- The owner's word: his sheet (id m4dk, name M4A1 다크나이트 — the v6.41 transcendence gun, which stays) made as a new weapon named M4A1 다크나이트-2, the design applied as written. id m4dk2; special (kind and buy-menu category), slot 1, hold rifle, a crate gun (WPN.crate, CRATE_GUNS — now nine, the same share of the 35 % —, the buy menu's special list for an account that owns one, lent in the range, never in 대인전), SHOP_NOTSOLD, gun_prices ('m4dk2', 0, not sold): run schema.sql (checked on Postgres 16 as an upgrade of v6.68, again, and fresh; an admin-set price elsewhere kept).
- Model: the sheet's 561 parts as data rows ([c, s, material, rx, rz, fillet or null = the engine's default, tag]), numbers unchanged (three decimals at most); tags chg (the charging handle, also the rear sight), mag (35 parts) and ghorn (the eye-and-horn structure: never moves). The sheet's five materials (DK2_MDEF: knGold brushed, knSpike noise, knLine brushed, knPlate brushed, knEye glow 232; gloss ≥ .3 → 248..254); 151 of 256 atlas slots. Points as the sheet (muzzle [0, .045, −.739], sup [−.005, −.01, −.33]); no pivots. View model 34.5k triangles; the world model m4dk2w the same parts as plain boxes (r 0: 6.7k). VM_POS: the M4A1 다크나이트's (not in the sheet).
- A: dmg 90, hs 150 / 90, limbK 50 / 90 (head 150 · body 90 · arms and legs 50), 780 rpm full auto (720 at 60 fps: the engine's frame steps), mag 50 + 1 (ch 1) / 300, spread [.006, .04, .11], rec [.017, .01], kb 3.4, stag .6, reload 2.5, draw .8, speed .9, shell (rifle), kickX the rifles' [.42, .6, .3] (the sheet: a slight lift). The sheet's buyDesc (W.bd).
- B (right click, W.alt 'dk2'): dk2Set — a.dk2T .25 s in which the actorWeapons hook clears fire and reload; then DK2.WB (dmg 180 → 300 / 180 / 100, rec [.006, .004], spread [.004, .03, .08], kickX × .35, sound dk2fireb) at 600 rpm (nextFire 60 / 600 − 1e−4: no dropped frame), two rounds a shot (noMag, then mag − 2; with one left B reloads: the hook shows the game an empty magazine for that frame and puts the round back, relCh kept). dk2Shot (tbShot hook, WB.rail + dk2p): rayCast to the first wall, dkLine (m4dk.js: head / body / legs / arms, the bosses' own volumes) up to 3 zombies, × .75 each next one, stag .6 on all, kb 3.4, a 퍽 (dk2pierce) where it goes on through; dk2Beam: a white core and a pale glow to where it stopped, faded by TB over .6 s. maxSpeed × .85. The view: Main.camera hook narrows to 52° over .2 s (the sheet's zoom [52] — right click is the mode switch, so not W.zoom). Measured: B 300 → 225 → 169 (heads) and 180 → 135 → 101 (bodies), the fourth untouched, stagger 1.1 s on all three; A 150 / 90 / 50.
- Flash (the sheet's sizes): the first-person flash scaled per shot (face .24 m → .05 / .04 m, side .2 m → .09 / .12 m), orange / white-yellow, its light with it (dk2Flash); in the world FX.muzzle is swapped for the shot (dk2Muzzle: a few sprites along the barrel).
- The eyes: the view model's material for this gun gets FS_VM with uGlow (glowing texels × 1.9 in B, over the .25 s switch: a.dk2K); third person: the actor's gun material uEmisA the same (ensureGun hook, back to 1 on any other gun). The structure itself never moves.
- Reload (rlBuild hook → rlDk2, the sheet's timeline over 2.5 s, either way: no extra charge from empty): the hand at the magazine and it out a little (0–.35 s), dropped and tumbling to the floor (.35–.8, gone there with a 쿵), a fresh one from the belt (.8–1.5), in (1.5–1.9, 찰칵), one tap (톡) and back (1.9–2.5). First draw: GUN_DESIGN charge, side u (the left), x .5, d .06, heavy .3, roll .15.
- Passive: the Deadshot +1 s (m4dk.js dkOwns wrapped: either Dark Knight, not two seconds for both; this one owned by the account or carried); dkOwnN names the one the note mentions.
- Network: ['dk2', id, 0 | 1]; a B shot is replayed from the 'f' event while that player's B is on; equip / turned / NY.clear / death put it back to A. Bots: B for a zombie over 10 m, back to A under 6 m (1.2 s apart). The weapon's name gets · B 관통 in B.
- Sounds (the sheet's words): dk2fire (the M4A1's, a .6 s tail), dk2fireb (the M249's, lower, .5 s, steady), dk2on / dk2off (a .25 s 우웅, the off one backwards), dk2pierce, dk2draw (leather and metal, the handle's 철컥 at .25 s, a low hum), dk2mrel / dk2mdrop / dk2min / dk2tap.
- Tests: studio renders (both sides, front, rear, top, the eyes, the muzzle, the player's view, the world model); first person: A and B (the eyes brighter), the flashes, the trail, the reload frame by frame, the first draw; unit: 12 shots a second in A, 50 → 51 on a reload with rounds left (2.5 s), the switch (nothing fired in it, .25 s), speed 4.64 → 3.94, the view 74 → 52 over .2 s, B 10 shots a second taking 20 rounds, the pierce numbers above, one round left → reload → 51, the Deadshot 5 → 6 s; a 150 s Mutation with every bot carrying it (23 switches, 404 B shots, the world model's eyes, reloads, no errors); 20 000 crate picks (each of the nine about 11 %). Multiplayer not tested here: the owner tests it.

## v6.68 — 「이태훈 딕건」 rebuilt to the owner's second design sheet (janus2.js rewritten; janus.js: per-weapon timings, calm; no server change)
- The owner's word: the sheet applied as written, nothing of ours changed in it. Its id / name (janus1 / 야누스-1) are the template's: the gun stays janus2 / 이태훈 딕건. The v6.55 build's own changes are gone (Christ moved back, the bridge, the longer barrel with a bore, our Mary). Only the id, the name, the cost, VM_POS, kickX, the blast radius, the chapter ×2.5, the HUD (ring, crosshair classes) and the grenade models are kept from before — things the sheet does not cover.
- Parts: the sheet's 304 parts as data rows ([c, s, material, rx, ry, rz, fillet, tag index, ax]), numbers unchanged (J2_P). Materials: the sheet's 14 (J2_MDEF: three tones, pattern brushed / engraved / glow / grain / noise / stripes, gloss, scratch — hood_black is defined but no part uses it); the old j2Blue / j2Gold / j2Green / j2Skin / j2Glow are gone. Alpha: glow 232; gloss ≥ .3 → max(248, 239 + 15 g) (lower would light itself in the view model); under .3 matte. 146 of 256 atlas slots.
- Points and hinge: the sheet's (muzzle [0, .03, −.21], pivots.break [0, .015, −.02] = G.hinge). Stats: dmg 120, rpm 60 single, mag 1, res 0, spread [.01, .04, .08], rec [.3, .08] (the game's largest: the next is .16), kb .8, stag .5, hs 1.5, reload 1, draw .9, speed .94; the first draw (break, x .3, d .15, side t, heavy .7, roll .4); buyDesc.
- res 0 with reloadType immediate = it loads itself: after every shot (mag 0, not in the B form, not drawing / frozen / pinned) the reload starts at once (VM.reload: rlBrk1 — the barrel breaks 45°, the case is lifted clear of the frame's face and flung back over the shoulder, the free hand brings a grenade from the belt to the chamber, the flick of the wrist); am.res is kept at 1 so a reload always has one; the HUD shows / ∞. The supply / ammo box / range top-ups of v6.55 are gone (nothing to top up).
- First-person rig (j2Rig): 'mag' = break_inner on the hinge; under it j2U (break_outer_up / top, scope_part), j2DL / j2DR (dn, bot), j2T (thorn_base, thorn_s, thornB: one mesh, each part's box built once about its centre and placed again whenever the growth changes, j2ThornSet), shell; the figures under j2U on the door [0, .07325, −.025]: j2J (Christ), j2E (his eyes, glow_white), j2EY (the same eyes in glow_jesus, with two additive halo sprites — the view model only), j2M (Mary and her eyes).
- The B form (VMX.janus2 / j2State): u from the right click; g (thorns, halves) = smooth(u / .5); f (the figures) = smooth((u − .3) / .55); 0.85 s in all (JNW oT), no firing until then (janus.js's lock), then JN.B 5 s. The halves: up +5 mm (with the rails, the sight, the figures), the lower ones ∓4 mm x, −5 mm y; thorns and vines by their root's height: t = (y − .030) / 9.5 mm clamped ±1 (the big thorns' roots sit at .041 / .019: they go whole with their half; the small ones near the line in between; the vines at .030 stay). thorn_s length × (1 + g), ax with it, the root fixed; thornB whole × g. The door: Christ −π f, Mary π − π f; Mary shows from f .5, Christ until f 1; the gold eyes from the signal until f .5. Closing (not in the sheet): the figures back over .55 s, then the thorns and halves over .5 s ending at .85 s (JNW cT). openK = f (the world model 'b' past 90°).
- The same shot either form: J2WB is WPN.janus2 with proj j2b, noMag only (same rec, kickX, spread, sound); j2Flame for both (a big muzzle flash, a fireball, smoke; the view model's flash larger and orange). The j2b grenade is the same model and speed; it does not count toward the signal (as JANUS-1). No violet: no light while open, no form-start burst / kick / shake (JNW calm, lit null), no jitter or tint in the form, no closing motes.
- Right click during the reload (the signal up) is kept (a.j2Q) and opens the form when the barrel shuts; a bot with the signal and a zombie in sight queues the same way (else its 1 s reloads would never leave it a free frame).
- World models: janus2n (Christ, white eyes, no thornB), janus2s (gold eyes), janus2b (the form baked at g = f = 1: Mary, thorns grown, halves apart); parts without fillets (3.2k triangles; the view model is 39k with the arms). The kill-feed / buy-menu icon draws janus2n.
- Sounds (the sheet's words): j2fire (a heavy blast, a 1 s tail), j2open (철컥), j2shut (텅), j2draw (kept), j2awake (wakes over 0.85 s: the halves unlock, the thorns grind out, the figures turn, a choir and a rush of air as it ends), j2end; j2fireb is gone.
- janus.js: jnOT / jnCT (JNW oT / cT, default JN.OPEN / JN.CLOSE) in jnOpen, jnRemote, jnOpenFx, jnClose; jnOpenK uses JNW openK if there is one; jnBStart and the world-model swap skip the burst, kick and shake when calm; the open light only with lit. JANUS-1 unchanged.
- Tests: studio renders of every state (A, signal, B, the flip at 45 / 90 / 135°, broken open, the world models); first person: idle, the reload frame by frame (the case clears the frame, the hand at the chamber), the first draw, the transition, B, closing; logic: one shot → reload at once, 6 shots in 6 s, B 0.85 s locked then 18 shots in 5 s with the magazine untouched, the form 6.7 s in all, five counted blasts → the signal, a right click mid-reload opens when it shuts, blast 120 at the centre (armour takes its half), 73 at 4.3 m, nothing at 4.5; a 150 s Mutation with every bot carrying it (24 forms opened, n / s / b models, no errors). Multiplayer not tested here: the owner tests it.

## v6.67 — the terms, the privacy policy and 회원 탈퇴 (legal/ → privacy.html · terms.html; account.js LEGAL / deleteAccount; shop.js ACCW 'del' / 'bye' and the sign-up box; schema.sql qz_account_delete)
